# Art rules: how Rosace gets drawn, like an artist would

This is the rulebook for constructing Rosace's face and figure by hand, in data, whether or not
the 3D base is good. It merges the three research files in `art-rules/` (face, figure, pixel)
into one working document: the construction method, the numbers at 144 px, the "never" rules,
and the self-check an artist runs before calling a drawing done. Every rule has an ID. The same
IDs are in `art-rules/checklist.json`, which says how each rule is checked: by a script metric
where one exists or can be built, otherwise by a critic's eye.

**Why this exists.** The 3D-to-pixel route stalled at about 5.7/10 over four critic rounds
(refs score 9). The critics kept naming the same things: a blank, half-lidded stare; a
mannequin stance; flat 3D banding; white-on-white legs; block hands; a flagpole grip; a missing
collar cross. Those are construction and craft failures, not render failures, so the fix is a
method an artist would use: guidelines first, trial and error, and explicit "negative" zones
where things must never be. The 3D render stays, but as a reference layer for perspective,
proportion and timing. It never decides the pose's attitude or the face.

**How the files fit together.**

| File | Owns |
|---|---|
| `ART-RULES.md` (this file) | the rules, their IDs, the method and the self-check |
| `art-rules/checklist.json` | how each rule is checked (metric, threshold, input, tool) |
| `art-rules/face.md` | the evidence behind the face rules: ref measurements, quotes, round-4 face violations with coordinates |
| `art-rules/figure.md` | the same for figure, pose, hands and grip |
| `art-rules/pose.md` | pose appeal for DESIGN revision 3.5 (the PS rules): S-curve, turn, stance, bust shape and read, hands and glaive framing, the back view, at 144 and 80, with its sources and the reconciliation with DESIGN 3.5 |
| `art-rules/finish-gap.md` | the finish gap in numbers: refs 07/08/09/04 against the integrated stills on chroma, value, outline, tones per material, clusters, banding, edges, head ratio and mass, each checked against a codec control (our still through the refs' WebP), with the ranked levers and their expected gains |
| `art-rules/pixel.md` | the same for rendering craft, plus the measurement commands |
| `DESIGN.md` | what she looks like (costume, palette, weapon); this file says how to draw it |
| `PIPELINE.md` 3.2, 3.10 | the stamp and override formats the construction output lands in |
| `tools/art-construct/README.md` | the tools that follow this method: face and figure construction, the rule checker, the review sheets |

When this file and a research file disagree, this file wins and the disagreement goes in
section 11 (open questions). When this file and `DESIGN.md` disagree on *what* she looks like,
`DESIGN.md` wins; on *how to draw it*, this file wins.

## Tags, units and rule IDs

Tags, as in every character doc:

- **[M]** measured, on the file named in the research doc the rule points to. Ref numbers are
  read off WebP-compressed art and are ±1 px (face) or ±2 px (figure, by eye) unless said
  otherwise. Rosace numbers from the round-4-fix renders are exact (lossless, palette-mapped).
- **[S key]** sourced from the tutorial or book with that key in section 12. "secondary" means
  we read a summary of the source, not the source.
- **[visible]** seen on an image, not measured.
- **[I]** our inference or proposal. **A number tagged [I] is a starting value to A/B, not a
  fact.** The learning log (section 10) is where those numbers get confirmed or changed.
- **[Dex]** Dex's direction or approved design fix (2026-09-29).

Units: **H** = her height, skull top to sole, weapon excluded = **144 px** (PIPELINE 2.1).
**h** = one head, skull top to chin = **24 px** at the current build (section 11 has an open
question on this). "Rows above the chin" count up from the chin row (row 0). Contrast is the
WCAG ratio `(L_hi + 0.05) / (L_lo + 0.05)`: 1.0 is identical, 1.3 barely separates at 1x, 2 is a
clear step, 4.5 is text contrast. Palette codes (OL, W1–W4, S1–S4, SB, G0–G4, I0–I4, A2–A5,
T2–T4, B1–B2) are `DESIGN.md` section 4 and `art/rosace/palette.json`.

Rule IDs: `AREA-Pnn` is a parameter (a target to hit), `AREA-Nnn` is a negative rule (a thing
that must not happen). Areas: **WF** workflow, **FG** figure and pose, **HD** hands, **GR** grip
and weapon, **CL** cloth and outfit, **FC** face, **HR** hair, **PX** pixel rendering, **PS**
pose appeal (appeal poses only: `idle_appeal*`, `back_appeal*`, the 80 px world idle; section 2.6).

Severity (also in the JSON):

- **block**: the drawing is rejected. Fix it before anyone reviews it.
- **major**: fix before a critic round unless the pose's notes give a reason.
- **minor**: polish. Allowed if it's deliberate and written down.

---

## 0. The short version

1. **Draw in the order an artist does:** gesture line, then masses, then the solid silhouette,
   then hands and weapon, then cloth, then the face, then the pixels. A failure goes back to the
   step that owns it; don't patch it in the pixels. [S proko-gesture, slynyrd-17, svarc-gun]
2. **Try several, keep the best.** 4–6 gesture thumbnails per pose, 3 variants per face
   expression, each scored and compared beside the refs at the same scale. [S saint11-1; I]
3. **Every pose has attitude:** shoulders and hips tilt opposite ways, one leg carries the
   weight, the head tilts, the arms do different jobs, and there's designed empty space between
   the arms and the body. [S london-contrapposto, aam-contrapposto, proko-proportions,
   thomas-johnston]
4. **Every face has a target and a mood:** both irises point at the same thing, brows are always
   there, the mouth has a bent corner, and at least one thing is asymmetric. The default face is
   bright and confident; half-lidded is one expression, not her resting face. [S csp-eyes,
   faigin, thomas-johnston; Dex]
5. **Hands are built, not stamped:** palm box, mitten of fingers, thumb wedge, wrist step. A
   gripping fist wraps the haft and the haft comes out collinear on the other side. [S
   proko-hands, svarc-gun]
6. **Pixels are placed, not inherited from the render:** one terminator per form, big first
   shadow steps, no banding, no pillow shading, and no two light materials touching at the same
   value. [S saint11-4, yu-mistakes, parmesan-aa, motomura-ggxrd]

---

## 1. The method (workflow rules)

This is the trial-and-error loop, written as steps the construction tools
(`tools/art-construct/`) follow. It's the same loop for a pose and for a face; the face just
runs it on a smaller grid.

| ID | Rule | Sev | Basis |
|---|---|---|---|
| WF-P01 | **Guides before pixels.** Every pose starts as a gesture wireframe (line of action as 3 control points; shoulder, hip and head axes as angles; weight foot; other foot; hand and grip points). Every face starts as a construction layer (head ball, side-plane oval, curved centre line, and the grid rows of FC-P01). The guide layer is data and never ships. | block | [S proko-gesture, proko-head, slynyrd-17] |
| WF-P02 | **The 3D render is a reference layer.** It supplies head and body position, turn and tilt, perspective, foreshortening, overlap order and timing. It never supplies the face, and it never decides attitude: where it disagrees with the wireframe (level shoulders, locked knees, upright head), the wireframe wins and the rig is re-posed or overridden. | block | [Dex; S han-wide for foreshortening and overlap; S motomura-ggxrd, vasseur-deadcells for the 3D-as-base trade-off] |
| WF-P03 | **Thumbnails.** 4–6 gesture wireframes per pose, filled solid at 1x and 3x and scored on the silhouette checks (FG-P13, FG-P14, FG-P11, FG-P08, FG-P09, GR-N02) and, from round R3, on the two checks a head or neck move can break (CL-P05, FG-P12). Keep the best two. *Changed in round R3: a cocked-head thumbnail scored first on the silhouette checks, then failed CL-P05 (block) and FG-P12 on the full build, as in round R2* | major | [S slynyrd-17, azzi-pixel-logic secondary; I for the counts; M rounds R2 and R3] |
| WF-P04 | **Face variants.** 3 variants per expression that differ on one axis only (eye height ±1, iris offset, brow angle, mouth corner). | major | [S saint11-1 "draw the same subject many times"; I] |
| WF-P05 | **Negative parts: keep-out and must-be zones.** Each pose and face file lists regions that must stay empty (arm gap, leg gap, weapon clearance, torso centre band, the gap between the eyes below the lash) and regions that must contain a thing (a fist at each grip, the collar cross, a dark value between the legs, the weight heel under the pit of the neck, brows, a mouth corner, the chin point). Scripts check both. | block | [Dex; S proko-proportions (negative shapes are designed); I] |
| WF-P06 | **Order of work:** gesture → masses → silhouette → hands and weapon → cloth → face → rendering. A failed check goes back to the step that owns it. | major | [S proko-gesture, slynyrd-17, svarc-gun] |
| WF-P07 | **Judge like an artist:** at 1x, 3x and 6x, beside the 07/08/09 crops at the same pixel scale, also blurred and in greyscale. | major | [S azzi-pixel-logic, steam-grey] |
| WF-P08 | **Flip test.** Mirror the whole drawing and look again; wonky construction jumps out when flipped. | minor | [I, common studio practice; no source read for it] |
| WF-P09 | **Write it down.** Keep the winner and the loser; record why in the file's `_doc` and in the learning log (section 10), including the rule that decided it. | major | [I] |
| WF-P11 | **The critic gate.** A face or a figure is not done until a whole-drawing pick beside the finish-bar ref, at x3 and 1x, answers a stranger's question ("which one would you pull for?"): candidates are whole faces or whole figures, shuffled, the last round's drawing among them as the control. No checker pass overrides the pick; a checker fail still blocks. *Added in round R3* | block | [M round R2 critique 3: 63/63 automatic face rules passed while the face missed the brief; Dex; S thomas-johnston (appeal), saint11-1 (side by side at one scale)] |
| WF-P10 | **Paint on the construction, in layers; measure the landmarks from the paint.** A head is painted as layers over its construction grid (back hair and neck, the face plane, front hair) plus per-expression feature overlays at anchors; a figure's high-detail parts (head, fist, cross) are painted or built per pose over the gesture. The checker's landmarks (eye boxes, iris, brows, mouth, contour) are read off the painted pixels, never assumed from the parameters. *Added in round R1* | major | [M round R1: the parametric C1 face read as a helmet with a boxy jaw; the painted head passes 52/52 automatic face rules; S slynyrd-29 "careful analysis, simplification through abstraction, and precise pixel placement"] |
| WF-P12 | **Paint shading in tone space.** A key frame's hand-painted shading shapes name a tone of a material (deep, shadow, lit, highlight), are masked to that material, and carry the render they were painted on (canvas, anchor, pose); a stale file is skipped, never smeared onto a new render. Colour-space paint over a render goes stale with any palette or preset change. *Added in shading round S1* | major | [M shading S1: `rosace_shade.paint`; I] |
| WF-P13 | **Judge against the current pose.** A blind judging set for pose or figure candidates includes the current shipped pose as a control, uses one letter mapping on every sheet (idle 144 and 80 at x3 and x1 beside the finish-bar refs, back views, black-fill silhouettes), labels panels with letters only, and records each letter's candidate and the render and pose file behind it in key.json. A render counts as current when its pose content, ignoring name and note, matches the pose file. *Added in figure-pose round FJ* | major | [M figure-pose FJ; I] |
| WF-P14 | **Integrate by one chain, then look for merge losses.** Lane passes reach the canonical stills through one ordered chain (`stills_v2.py`, picks in `art/rosace/integrated.json`): render and passes, post (+ shading stage), hair pass, face, glyphs and rim, post-face passes, outfit passes, hands, and the palette remap last, so every pass reads palette codes. Before a merge counts, a not-blind look sheet sets the integrated still beside each lane's own pick at x3 (144) and x4 (80), and every visible difference is either explained by another lane's change or fixed. A lane step that breaks a rule only in combination is switched off in the integrated config with its reason, never silently. *Added in the Integrate step* | major | [M Integrate: the brow windows and fringe tips were fine on the hair lane's own stills and broke on the integrated ones; the hue trial went grey when shifted from the remapped indigo] |
| WF-P15 | **World-scale sheets and an honest shuffle.** A whole-character blind set shows the 80 px world still beside the same ref crops resampled so the ref figure is 80 px tall (box filter, then snapped to the native crop's own palette, no dither), with the factor and the method in key.json, and the 144 still beside the refs at their native grid. The letter shuffle must move every entry off its listed place and keep ours and the control apart; the seed actually used goes in key.json. A resampled ref is an approximation of the ref at that size, not a drawing, so an 80 px verdict of 'ours reads better' is read against 05 (drawn at our size) before it counts. *Added in whole round WH1* | major | [M WH1: seed 20260929 put ours on A beside the control on B; the resampled refs speckle and go muddy at 0.51-0.58x (07, 08 and 09 are lossy-sourced) [visible x3]; I] |
| WF-P16 | **Fair presentation and a kept verdict.** On a whole-character blind sheet, ours stands on its contact shadow (`still_ground.png`) wherever the ref crops show their painted ground shadow; once per whole round, one extra diagnostic sheet shows ours through the same lossy WebP round trip as the refs (quality 90) so the critics' finish score can be split into art and codec (that sheet never counts as progress). Each critic's sheet (per-parameter scores and notes) is saved beside key.json. *Added in round FG1* | minor | [M FG1: 07/08/09 are lossy WebP; our 34-colour idle through WebP q90 measures 5,022 colours, 43 colour groups at tol 16 (refs 32-46) and 7.5 colours per 3x3 flat patch (refs 8.6-8.9); round-2 left no written verdicts on disk; finish-gap.md] |
| WF-P17 | **One chain for every entry of a cross-route set.** When routes built at different times are judged in one blind set, every entry goes through the same post passes except the axis its route changes: the contact shadow is the chain's own `still_ground` spec (drawn from the entry's boot ids when its lane skipped the step), and any pass that one entry has and another lacks (for example the weapon pass) is listed under `confounds` in key.json with the pixels it touches. *Added in the finish judging set FJ1* | minor | [M FJ1: F1 P4 had an ellipse shadow of its own, F1/F2 lacked author_hands' weapon pass on the appeal idle and back (182 / 290 glaive px at 144 vs current); the redrawn ground is pixel-identical to the chain's on all 6 current stills] |
| WF-P18 | **A native small ref before any 80 px call.** An 80 px sheet carries at least one sprite drawn at that size (ref 05 at its own 1x, 99-102 px tall, in the frame closest to the pose), beside the resampled crops; an "ours reads better at 80" verdict counts only if it holds against that native ref. A white-ground ref is keyed by a flood fill from the box border, never by a global white key, so no backdrop shows inside the figure. *Added in round D9; changed in round D9-R1 (old: no keying rule, frame 0 for every shot)* | major | [critic FJ1: "the 80 px ref crops (box-resample plus 32-colour median-cut) turn the refs to mush and flatter the clean toon at 80"; WF-P15; M D9-R1: the global key removed 107-281 px from inside each ref 05 figure] |
| WF-P19 | **Another lane's files stay read-only; a round's edits are a patch.** A whole-character round changes the model and the poses at render time from its own patch file (`drive9/r2_model.json`: mesh edits, added parts, pose patches deep-merged over the owner's pose by name). It never edits the owner's pose, shape or blend files. key.json records the patch and lists it as a confound. The previous round's stills sit in the blind set as their own letter (`prev`), so the critics can judge whether the round moved. *Added in round D9-R2* | major | [task rule: the figure-pose lane's files are read-only while it runs; M D9-R2: 3 pose patches, 2 model edits, recorded in key.json `sources.r2`, 8 letters with `prev`] |

**What the 3D is still for** [Dex]: once animations exist, the render gives timing, the
perspective of a turning head or a foreshortened thrust, and where each limb sits in depth. A
constructed pose uses the render's joint positions as a starting point for its wireframe, then
bends them to the rules below.

---

## 2. Figure, gesture and pose (FG)

### 2.1 Principles

- **One line of action, curves against straights.** Find one curve from the head to the toes;
  use only straight, C and S lines; mirrored curves make "a snowman". Offset the apexes of
  curves; avoid parallel forms, because they "create closure". [S proko-gesture,
  hampton-gesture]
- **Contrapposto.** The shoulders tilt against the pelvis; the weight leg is vertical from the
  hip joint to the footprint; the torso pinches on one side and stretches on the other; the
  centre of gravity sits over the weight foot. [S london-contrapposto, aam-contrapposto,
  proko-bean]
- **Three masses on one spine** (head, rib cage, pelvis) that tilt, lean and twist
  independently; in a twist the centre line becomes an S. The female rib cage is narrower and
  the pelvis wider. [S proko-bean, proko-robobean]
- **Negative shapes are designed.** The hole between an arm and the body is a shape to design,
  not leftover. [S proko-proportions]
- **No twins.** Left and right mirroring each other looks lifeless; appeal is charisma; stage
  one idea per pose. [S thomas-johnston, riki-twinning]
- **Idles show personality.** A good idle tells you about the character; a few held poses with
  uneven timing read richer than an even loop. [S gamedev-idle, slynyrd-52]

### 2.2 Proportions at 144 px

Heights up from the sole unless it says y (y is from the skull top, y = 0).

| ID | Landmark | Target at 144 | Sev | Basis |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| FG-P01 | Head count | 6.0 heads (band 5.5–6.5); h = 24 px skull top to chin | major | [M refs 5.7–6.3 with 24–25 px heads; S aam-ratio, slynyrd-17] |
| FG-P02 | Crotch and hanging wrists | on the midpoint: 72 px above the sole (±2) | major | [S aam-ratio] |
| FG-P03 | Knee centre | 38–40 px above the sole (thigh = calf) | major | [S london-contrapposto, aam-ratio] + arithmetic |
| FG-P04 | Elbow (arm hanging) | at the waist, y ≈ 58; chest/abdomen/pelvis thirds end at y ≈ 45 / 58 / 72 | minor | [S aam-ratio] + arithmetic |
| FG-P05 | Shoulders / waist / hips, three-quarter | 27 / 15 / 24 px; rib cage reads narrower than the pelvis | major | [DESIGN 2 × 1.125; S proko-robobean] |
| FG-P06 | Neck width | 8–10 px | minor | [I; realistic 1/2–2/3 h per S slynyrd-17, slimmer for an anime woman] |

### 2.3 Pose parameters (idle and hero stills)

The refs' numbers set the targets; figure.md 3.2 has the ref-by-ref table and ours.

| ID | Parameter | Target at 144 | Sev | Basis |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| FG-P07 | Line of action | one C or S from crown to weight heel; sagitta (how far the curve bows from its chord) 6–12 px in an idle, 15–30 px in a strike | major | [S proko-gesture; I for the px] |
| FG-P08 | Shoulder axis | tilted 4–10°: a 2–5 px drop across 27 px | block | direction [S london-contrapposto, aam-contrapposto]; size [I] |
| FG-P09 | Hip axis | tilted the opposite way to the shoulders, 4–10°: a 2–4 px drop across 24 px | block | direction [S london-contrapposto]; size [I] |
| FG-P10 | Legs | weight leg within 5° of vertical, hip to heel; free knee bent 10–30°, its knee 1–3 px lower than the weight knee | block | [S london-contrapposto; I for the numbers] |
| FG-P11 | Balance | a plumb line from the pit of the neck lands inside the weight foot's footprint, ±2 px (unless falling, leaping or pushing off on purpose) | major | [S london-contrapposto, aam-contrapposto, secondary for the plumb-line phrasing] |
| FG-P12 | Head | tilted 5–15° off the neck axis in idle and hero poses, chin 1 px down or turned toward the viewer or target | major | [I, from S thomas-johnston (appeal)] |
| FG-P13 | Arm–torso gaps in a solid fill | at least 1 in an idle, at least 2 in a hero or strike pose; each ≥ 3 px wide at its narrowest and ≥ 30 px² | block | [S proko-proportions; I for the numbers; ours was 0, M] |
| FG-P14 | Width | widest body without the weapon ≥ 0.40 H (58 px) | major | [M refs 0.38–0.55, ours 0.28; I target] |
| FG-P15 | Feet apart, centre to centre | combat idle 40–56 px; the demure processional stance 20–30 px only with a bent free knee | major | [M refs 44–57, ours 28; I] |
| FG-P16 | Arm asymmetry | elbow angles differ by ≥ 30°; hand heights by ≥ 8 px | major | [I, from S thomas-johnston] |
| FG-P17 | Twist | when the rib cage and pelvis turn differently, the centre line is an S, and the twisted rib cage shows more of one side plane in its shadow tone | minor | [S proko-bean, proko-robobean] |
| FG-P18 | Strikes | the spine leans into the thrust along the line of action; foreshortened near parts are bigger and overlap | major | [S proko-gesture, svarc-gun, han-wide] |

### 2.4 Idle motion (Slynyrd's 92 px idle scaled to 144, × 1.57)

| ID | Part | Target | Sev | Basis |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| FG-P19 | Idle loop offsets | head and torso 1–2 px; shoulders, elbows and outer hand 3 px; inner or free hand 6 px; knees dip 1–2 px at the low point; 8 frames, 1–4 at 100 ms and 5–8 at 50 ms (for her serene idle, 5–8 at 75 ms) | minor | [S slynyrd-52 + arithmetic; I for the slower half] |
| FG-P20 | Sub-pixel motion and a signature beat | motion under a pixel is done by shifting colour along an edge; the staff turn (DESIGN 1) is three held poses with uneven timing, not an even rotation | minor | [S saint11-idle secondary, gamedev-idle] |
| FG-P21 | Head size on the appeal stills | hair top to chin ÷ hair top to sole 0.18-0.20 on the standing stills (idle and back, 144 and 80; N1's crouch is exempt), which is 27-29 px at 144. If the pose's tilt drops the head under 0.18, scale the head with `finish_f2` (1.05-1.15) rather than redraw the face. Never over 0.20: past ref 07 it reads big-headed. *Added in finish route RF2* | major | [M RF2: appeal idle 0.181 (25.7 px) at head 1.00, 0.195 at 1.10, 0.201 at 1.15, 0.208 at 1.20; refs 07/08/09/04 0.20/0.18/0.17/0.14 (finish-gap.md 2.5); by eye (blind to the key) 1.10 won the idle set and 1.20 came last] |

### 2.5 Figure negative rules

| ID | Never / avoid | Why | Sev | Basis |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| FG-N01 | **Never** level shoulders over level hips in a standing pose | the textbook stiff pose; a matched pair of level lines is a twin: the mannequin | block | [S london-contrapposto, aam-contrapposto, thomas-johnston] |
| FG-N02 | **Never** lock both knees in an idle | the weight looks even and the pose goes static | block | [S london-contrapposto] |
| FG-N03 | **Never** mirror the arms or the legs | twins look lifeless | major | [S thomas-johnston] |
| FG-N04 | **Never** close an arm against the body in a key pose | the arm vanishes into the silhouette | block | [S proko-proportions; QUALITY-RUBRIC 1] |
| FG-N05 | **Never** put the plumb line outside the supporting foot unless she's falling, leaping or pushing off | she looks like she's tipping | major | [S london-contrapposto, aam-contrapposto] |
| FG-N06 | **Never** leave a 1–2 px sliver of background between two parts (haft and leg, sleeve and haft, hair and shoulder). Separate by ≥ 4 px or overlap by ≥ 3 px | a tangent: a visual mistake | major | [S blevins-tangents] |
| FG-N07 | **Avoid** a head held perfectly upright and facing straight out in idle and hero poses | attitude lives in the head–neck angle | major | [I, from S thomas-johnston] |
| FG-N08 | **Avoid** a torso that stays upright while the legs lunge | no line of action through the spine | major | [S proko-gesture, svarc-gun] |
| FG-N09 | **Avoid** exaggeration held back: if a critic could call it stiff, push the tilts and the line of action one step further and A/B both | a perfect copy of reality looks static | minor | [S proko-gesture, thomas-johnston] |

### 2.6 Pose appeal (PS): DESIGN revision 3.5

`art-rules/pose.md` holds the evidence, the tables at 144 and 80, the keep-out zones (4.3), the
reconciliation with DESIGN 3.5 (5) and the self-check (6). These rules apply to **appeal poses
only** (`idle_appeal*`, `back_appeal*`, the 80 px world idle). There PS-P01 replaces FG-P08 and
FG-P09's 4–10° band. Every other FG, HD and GR rule still applies. sw = one shoulder width (27 px
at 144, 15 px at 80). `figure_pose.py -- check` (round FP2) computes the rig-measurable rules:
PS-P01, P04–P09, N01–N04, N13 and N14 in full, and P03 and N05 in part. The fill-based rules
(PS-P02, P10, P16, P17, N07, N10) still print SKIP.

| ID | Parameter | Target (144; 80) | Sev | Basis |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| PS-P01 | S-curve tilts | shoulders 8–14° and hips 8–14° the opposite way; at 80 drops of 2–4 px and 2–3 px | block | [Dex; S proko-soul, aam-contrapposto] |
| PS-P02 | Weight hip pushed out | 3–6 px past the rib-cage contour; 2–3 px | major | [S proko-soul; I] |
| PS-P03 | Chest lift, back arch | rib cage pitched back 5–12° against the pelvis; back curve 2–4 px; 1–2 px | major | [S pinup-studio, gurney-elvgren; I] |
| PS-P04 | Turn chain | pelvis 35–60°, chest 30–50°, head 10–30° from the camera, each less than the one below; eyes on the viewer | major | [S modelscamp, pinup-studio] |
| PS-P05 | Head | tilted 5–10° against the shoulder line; chin 1 px down | major | [Dex; S proko-soul, vilppu-manual, durand-1x] |
| PS-P06 | Stance | heels 1.3–1.6 sw apart (35–43 px; 20–24 px); knee gap ≤ 0.6 × heel gap (the λ) | block | [Dex; S csp-cheishiru-legs] |
| PS-P07 | Weight leg | ≤ 5° off vertical, knee ≤ 5°, ankle under the pit of the neck ±2 px; ±1 px | block | [Dex; S london-contrapposto, loomis-fdaiw] |
| PS-P08 | Free leg | knee 10–30°, heel up 3–8 px (2–4), turned out 15–40°, forward of the weight foot | major | [Dex; S lazypencil, modelscamp] |
| PS-P09 | Thigh splay | ≤ 20° between the thigh axes | block | [S csp-cheishiru-legs; I] |
| PS-P10 | Bust silhouette break | 7–12 px past the underbust on the profile side; 4–7 px | major | [Dex; M mesh: 7.5 at yaw 30 now] |
| PS-P11 | Bust shape | apex 0.40–0.55 upper arm below the shoulder; upper slope ≥ 1.5 × lower curve; ≥ 3 px flat chest under the collarbone | major | [S aam-breasts-1, aam-breasts-2, loomis-fdaiw; M] |
| PS-P12 | Bust read | 1–2 px underbust shadow over ≥ 60 % width, one 2–5 px highlight, 1–3 tension lines; at 80 one of each at most | major | [Dex; S slynyrd-57, csp-jozlix, hampton-folds] |
| PS-P13 | Bust consistency | apex ±1 px against the rib cage across poses | major | [CRITIQUE-PARAMS 25] |
| PS-P14 | Free hand | on the weight hip, collar or hair; wrist 15–40°; side of the hand; fingertip contact; elbow window ≥ 3 px | major | [Dex; S aam-contrapposto, durand-1x, modelscamp] |
| PS-P15 | Glaive hand and angle | grip between the apex row and 1 h over the shoulder line; glaive 15–35° leaning away | major | [Dex; GR-P09] |
| PS-P16 | Curves kept clear | nothing in the bust-break or hip-break bands; haft away from the bust profile; blade ≥ 4 px clear of the head | major | [Dex; S blevins-tangents] |
| PS-P17 | Negative windows | an arm window and a leg window, each ≥ 30 px²; ≥ 8 px² | major | [S modelscamp; FG-P13] |
| PS-P18 | Back view | glance over the shoulder, hip shifted 3–6 px, weight leg straight, 2–5 px spine curve, ≥ 60 % open back visible, strap one 1 px line | major | [Dex; S proko-soul, loomis-fdaiw] |
| PS-P19 | Trial and blind pick | 3–6 one-axis variants per appeal pose (stance, tilts, turn, bust size against prominence), picked blind beside 07/08/09/04 at 3x and 1x, both sizes | block | [WF-P04, WF-P11; Dex] |
| PS-P20 | Where cheats live | per-pose cheats in `figure_pose.py`; body shape only in `figure_shape.py`, the same in every pose | minor | [S motomura-ggxrd; I] |
| PS-P21 | Garments follow the bust | every chest piece (bodice with its window and halter, collar cross, plate) carries the body's `figure_bust` key; posed: ≤ 10 new vertices behind the skin against the base, 0 skin islands inside the bodice at 144 and 80 | major | [M figure-pose FP1; I] |
| PS-P22 | The pose applier round-trips | a pose file with no `figure` block comes out of `figure_pose.py` exactly as out of `posing.py` (every bone matrix within 1e-5; `figure_pose.py -- roundtrip`) | block | [M figure-pose FP2: max diff 0.0 on all four poses] |

| ID | Never / avoid | Why | Sev | Basis |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| PS-N01 | **Never** a crotch-forward or splayed stance (pelvis < 20° from the camera with heels > 1.2 sw and knee ÷ heel gap > 0.8, or thighs splayed > 25°) | vulgar, not elegant | block | [Dex; S lazypencil, csp-cheishiru-legs] |
| PS-N02 | **Never** a symmetric stance (feet at the same depth and angle, soles on one row, both knees straight) | the mannequin twin | block | [Dex; S thomas-johnston] |
| PS-N03 | **Never** parallel feet (axes within 10°) | kills the λ | major | [Dex; S lazypencil] |
| PS-N04 | **Never** both knees locked (FG-N02 restated) | static | block | [FG-N02; Dex] |
| PS-N05 | **Never** hunched or shrugged shoulders (≥ 4 px of neck on one side; 2 at 80); the rib cage never pitched forward | timid, not lifted | major | [Dex; S pinup-studio] |
| PS-N06 | **Never** a fist on a hand at rest | tension where grace should be | major | [Dex; S modelscamp] |
| PS-N07 | **Never** hands, sleeves, hair or the glaive in the bust-break or hip-break bands | hides the curves | major | [Dex] |
| PS-N08 | **Never** realistic sag (apex past 0.60 upper arm, or the lower curve longer than the upper slope) | DESIGN 3.5 bans it | major | [Dex; S aam-breasts-2] |
| PS-N09 | **Never** a sphere stuck on (equal slopes with a top notch, or no flat chest under the collarbone) | "totally round and hard looking" | major | [Dex; S aam-breasts-1, hampton-fddi] |
| PS-N10 | **Never** a 1–2 px tangent along the bust or hip contour | the mistake sits where the eye rests | major | [S blevins-tangents; FG-N06] |
| PS-N11 | **Never** both knees or both feet turned in | timid; only the free knee turns in | major | [S csp-cheishiru-legs; I] |
| PS-N12 | **Never** an idle hanging arm, or a hand pressed flat on the body | "hands hanging motionless"; flat hands look stuck | major | [S loomis-fdaiw, pinup-studio, modelscamp] |
| PS-N13 | **Never** the chest square-on to the camera (< 20°) | the widest, flattest view; the bust break vanishes | major | [S modelscamp; Dex] |
| PS-N14 | **Avoid** the chin lifted in three-quarter view | haughty, not knowing | minor | [S modelscamp] |
| PS-N15 | **Avoid** lines that steer the eye to the crotch | eye control: the face and the bust–waist curve first | major | [S kearns-elvgren; Dex] |
| PS-N16 | **Avoid** more skin as the lever | "more SUGGESTIVE than EXPLICIT" | major | [S lazypencil; Dex] |
| PS-N17 | **Never** more bust detail at 80 than one shadow, one highlight, one line | noise at 80 | minor | [S slynyrd-57; I] |
| PS-N18 | **Never** change the bust size between poses | reads as a model swap | major | [CRITIQUE-PARAMS 25] |
| PS-N19 | **Avoid** a stance where no glaive angle clears both thighs by 10° (GR-N02, O-23): put the haft on the weight-leg side at 15–20°, or outside the free foot at ≥ its thigh angle + 10° | a conflict the pose creates | major | [GR-N02; I] |
| PS-N20 | **Never** an upper-pole shelf: a one-row step of more than 3.5 px (144) in the side profile's upper slope | the top of the bust reads as a ledge under the collar; a lifted, projected bust makes it first | major | [M figure-pose FP1: base 2.6, lift+projection 4.65; FP2: S5 (lift + upper fill) 2.92; I threshold] |
| PS-N21 | **Never** keep a pose whose IK misses: a foot or hand target more than 5 mm from its bone (figure_pose.py check, `ik_miss_mm`) | the limb didn't reach; the check's numbers describe a pose that isn't drawn | block | [M figure-pose FPA: 24-92 mm foot misses at heel gaps ≥ 34 px] |
| PS-P23 | Back-view stance: the free leg re-aimed into the back camera's picture plane; feet not stacked (≤ 150° apart on screen), heels ≥ 25 px apart at 144 (≥ 14 at 80) | the idle's λ seen from behind is one column | major | [M figure-pose FPA B1-B8; I] |
| PS-P24 | Reach budget for a planted, leaning glaive: with the pit of the neck over the weight ankle (PS-P07), the haft's closest 3D approach to the gripping shoulder joint is ≤ 0.47 m (upper + lower arm 0.48 m, grip 5 cm off the wrist), so the grip gap stays ≤ 15 mm (GR-P11). On this body that means a lean ≤ 20° with the butt just outside the weight boot, or up to 25° only with the butt at or under her toe (the haft then crosses the weight shin) | a lean the arm can't reach leaves the hand off the haft or forces the pit off the ankle | major | [M figure-pose FC: 0.53-0.70 m at 22-25° outside the boot, 0.40-0.45 m at 20° outside or 25° at the toe] |
| PS-P26 | Grip height against lean for a glaive planted on the weight side: with the pit of the neck over the weight ankle, a head-height grip (hand at the chin row, grip about 0.9 H up) caps the screen lean at about 16° (elbow soft); 18° needs the grip at or under about 0.875 H with the arm straight, and 20° a grip near shoulder height (about 0.82 H) with the butt just behind the weight heel. Pick the grip height or the angle; a brief that asks for both at their top values can't be met | a lean-away past the budget leaves the hand off the haft (PS-N21) or forces a locked, straight arm | major | [M figure-pose FR1: grip z 1.70 m / 16° elbow 39°; 1.66 / 18° elbow 3°; 1.60 / 20° 23 mm miss; 1.55 / 20° behind the heel, elbow 14°; H 1.896 m] |
| PS-P27 | A bell sleeve on a raised arm hangs: drape g ≥ 0.8 with little wind, so it falls below the forearm as one vertical shape outside the haft | blown back (g 0.3) it lies along the arm as a horizontal white slab that reads as a bar or a wing at 144 | minor | [M figure-pose FR1 V1 against V6, visible x3 and x1] |
| PS-N23 | **Never** leave the free hand's sleeve beside the bust on the bust-profile side: a far hand at the collar or lips takes its elbow up and back (pole behind the shoulder) and its sleeve blown back behind the hair | with the elbow out to the side, the white cuff and sleeve sit on the bust's top contour and merge with the bodice white into one mass at 80 | major | [M figure-pose FR1: V2/V8 against V12/V13, visible x3 and x1] |
| PS-P28 | Balance: she stands over her weight foot and curves inside the body, she doesn't lean as one slab. At 144 the pit of the neck is within 3 px of the weight ankle (80: 2 px), the torso axis (pit of the neck to the pelvis centre) within 6° of vertical, and the principal axis of the whole black-fill silhouette without the weapon within 12° | round 1's idle had the pit exactly over the ankle and a 1.4° torso, yet read as 'falling onto the staff': the raised glaive arm, sleeve and head went one way and the free leg the other, a 18.6° silhouette axis. The control A (6.4°) read grounded | major | [M figure-pose FR2: `refine/round-2/axis.py`; critic FR1 idle 6/10] |
| PS-P29 | Bust read from value: a cleavage wedge along the bodice's normal flip between the apexes (the side facing away from the key light, 2 px at the top, 1 px below, reaching the neckline), an underbust shadow on the first pixel under the bodice, and a one-step darker underside where the bodice faces down; the shape key is unchanged | the refs' busts read from value, ours read as a white pillow or ruff | major | [critic FJ1 (Dex 3.5 item 4 fix); M D9: the normal x flips by 0.7-1.1 across the valley on 12 rows of the idle and Q at 144; none on N1 and the back] |
| PS-P30 | A hand "on the hip" goes on the hip the camera sees | a wrist set on the rest-frame hip (world position beside the hip joint) lands in front of the belly in a 45° view and hides under the cuff. The critics read that as "a hand clump at the sternum". Put the wrist out, back and down onto the hip's silhouette edge in the shot's own view, with the elbow pole out and back, so the forearm and the waist frame a gap; check it by eye on the x3 still | major | [critic D9-R1 body lens 6; M D9-R2: rest wrist [-0.15, 0, 1.22] → [-0.21, 0.09, 1.10], pole [-0.9, 1.0, 1.3], trials r2_vd / r2_ve] |
| PS-P31 | Tilting the weapon keeps the grip in reach | a planted glaive is placed from its butt, so tilting its direction swings the grip points away from the hands; past a small tilt the arms can no longer reach and she lets go of it. Tilt it only as far as both hands stay on the haft (check the x3 still), and move the body instead when the critique asks for more angle. *Added in round D9-R3* | major | [critic D9-R2: "Q ... a vertical glaive covering her body; angle the glaive 15-35 degrees off the body"; trials D9-R3 on Q: direction x +0.5 (about 25°) left both hands off the haft, x -0.3 crossed her whole body, x +0.18 (about 10°) keeps the grip and clears the face] |

## 3. Hands (HD)

**Construction** [S proko-hands, proko-handbones]: the palm is a box about as wide as it is
tall. The fingers are grouped as a mitten; any finger that separates gets drawn on its own. The
thumb base is a triangular box that can be stretched, squished and rotated. There's a small step
down from the forearm to the hand at the wrist. The whole hand is about as long as the face; the
knuckles sit halfway from the wrist to the middle fingertip, on a curved line.

At pixel scale the mitten is the whole trick [I]: a fist is one mass plus one thumb wedge; an
open hand is one mitten plus at most one or two separated fingers.

| ID | Hand | Construction and pixels at 144 | Sev | Basis |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| HD-P01 | Every hand | palm box + mitten + thumb wedge + 1 px wrist step on the side the hand bends toward; built per pose from that construction, not picked from a fixed library of blocks | block | [S proko-hands] |
| HD-P02 | Fist | 7–9 × 6–8 px; thumb wedge 2–3 px; a 1 px darker line (S3 or S4) under the knuckle row, curved, middle knuckle highest | major | [S proko-hands, proko-handbones; I px; refs' hands 8–10 px across by eye, M ±2] |
| HD-P03 | Open, relaxed | 10–12 px long; at most 2 visible finger separations; fingertip line curves, middle finger longest; thumb apart | major | [S proko-hands, proko-handbones; I px] |
| HD-P04 | Hand on hip, or at the collar | 8–10 px; back of the hand shows; mitten bent at the knuckles; wrist in line with the forearm within 30° | minor | [S proko-hands, ryu1991; I px] |
| HD-P05 | Tones | 3 skin tones + 1 px line; shadow on the palm side, light on the knuckle plane (one light direction, not a ring) | major | [S yu-mistakes; I, within S1–S4] |
| HD-P06 | Wrist limits | ≤ 40° ulnar, ≤ 17° radial, ≤ 60° extension, ≤ 54° flexion; stay near 40° for anything that must read natural | major | [S ryu1991] |
| HD-P07 | Fist on a 3 px haft | the wrist step sits on the back of the fist (not a forearm's end away) and the thumb wedge stays inside the fist's outline, so the fist is 8–9 px across with ≥ 2 px either side of the haft band: HD-P02 and GR-P06 hold together. *Added in round R1* | minor | [M round R1: a proud thumb or a far wrist step made the fist 10 px across while one side stayed under 2 px; S proko-hands for the wrist step] |
| HD-P08 | Fists read on every attack | each gripping fist shows (≥ 20 px of hand at 144, ≥ 8 at 80; two fists on an attack) and is separated from the belt gold, the hair and the sleeve by 1 px of outline: no hand pixel touches one of them unless one of the two is OL. *Added in round G2* | major | [M round G2 critique 8: the N1 rear hand dissolved into the gold belt, N2's into the hair, the idle's into the white bell; round 1's pick fails it on 7 of 8 stills, the G2 pick on none] |

| ID | Never / avoid | Why | Sev | Basis |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| HD-N01 | **Never** draw a fist as a rectangle with no thumb wedge or knuckle direction | it reads as a block | block | [S proko-hands] |
| HD-N02 | **Avoid** an even row of four finger bumps on an open hand | a comb or a crown, not a hand | major | [S proko-hands (mitten); I] |
| HD-N03 | **Never** pillow-shade a hand (light centre, dark ring all round) | kills the light direction | major | [S yu-mistakes, saint11-4] |
| HD-N04 | **Avoid** 1 px fingers that carry shading | a 1 px part can't hold light and shadow; it turns to specks | minor | [S yu-mistakes] |
| HD-N05 | **Never** bend a wrist past HD-P06 unless it's a smear or impact frame where the break is the point | reads as broken | major | [S ryu1991] |

## 4. Grip and weapon (GR)

**Principles.** Build the pose from the whole body first, then let the weapon's weight change
the spine, shoulders and hips; the common mistakes are soft hands, broken wrists, a weightless
weapon, and hands, weapon and body drawn as separate pieces [S svarc-gun]. People who fight with
polearms put the rear hand near the butt in front of the lower belly or at the hip, and the
front hand about a foot up [S digrassi]; in the naginata mid-guard the hands are shoulder-width
apart and the trailing hand sits at the hip crease [S chudan].

| ID | Parameter | Target at 144 (haft 3 px thick) | Sev | Basis |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| GR-P01 | Hand spacing, ready or thrust | 24–30 px | major | [S digrassi ("about one foot" = 0.185 H at 165 cm = 27 px), chudan (shoulder width)] + arithmetic |
| GR-P02 | Hand spacing, wide sweep (N5) | up to 40 px | minor | [I] |
| GR-P03 | Hand spacing, twirl (N3, Q) | both hands within 8–14 px of the haft's middle | minor | [I] |
| GR-P04 | Rear hand | in front of the lower belly, at the hip, or at the hip crease | major | [S digrassi, chudan] |
| GR-P05 | Haft through the fists | both fist centres on the haft line ±1 px; the haft disappears behind the fingers and comes out collinear on the other side | block | [I; DESIGN 7] |
| GR-P06 | Fist on the haft | ≥ 7 px tall: 2 px of knuckles above the haft, 2 px of fingers below; the thumb wedge crosses the haft on the near side | major | [I, from S proko-hands] |
| GR-P07 | Two forward hands | knuckle rows face the same way | minor | [I] |
| GR-P08 | Weight in the body | shoulder carry: that shoulder rises 1–2 px. Low hold: that shoulder drops 1–3 px and the torso leans 3–8° away to counterweight | major | direction [S svarc-gun]; numbers [I] |
| GR-P09 | Rest angle, from vertical | processional idle 15–35°, leaning away from her body (DESIGN 1); shoulder carry 40–60° | block | [M refs 16–56°, ours 6.5°; Dex; I for the range] |
| GR-P10 | Clearance | off the grip, the haft is 4–8 px clear of the body or overlaps it by ≥ 3 px | major | [DESIGN 1; S blevins-tangents] |
| GR-P11 | The grip is real in 3D | on a rendered drawing every gripping hand's grip point sits within 1.5 cm of its socket (about 1 px at 144) before any pixel closes it. *Added in round G1* | major | [M round G1: N1 12.5 cm, Q 11.6, N2 5.0; idle leans past 18° 6–39 cm] |
| GR-P12 | Disc facing at rest | the flats face the viewer (disc facing ≥ 0.85), so the cross arms and the rose read as a processional cross. *Added in round G1* | minor | [M round G1: the R3 idle's disc faced 0.47 and read as a gold blob; DESIGN 7] |
| GR-P13 | Smear on the contact frame | a contact frame (N1 contact) carries an authored smear arc behind the blade: a crescent about the pivot fist, a sharp A5 outer edge, tapering to nothing over 25–45°, over the background (≥ 150 px at 144, ≥ 45 at 80). *Added in round G2* | minor | [M round G2 critique 7 and top fix 5; refs 07, 09 attack, 10 (smear sheet)] |

| ID | Never / avoid | Why | Sev | Basis |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| GR-N01 | **Never** let the weapon float: at least one hand wraps the haft (except N4's vault and Q's plant) | a weightless prop | block | [S svarc-gun; DESIGN 7] |
| GR-N02 | **Never** run the haft within 10° of parallel to the torso, a leg or an arm without a clear overlap | parallels create closure; a near-vertical staff beside a vertical body is the flagpole | block | [S hampton-gesture] |
| GR-N03 | **Avoid** the weapon crossing the torso's centre band (±3 px of the centre line, chest to crotch) in an idle | hides the collar cross, chest window and tabard; flattens the silhouette | major | [I, QUALITY-RUBRIC 1] |
| GR-N04 | **Avoid** a straight line from the blade tip through the arm to the far shoulder (the coat hanger); break it at the elbow or wrist | the weapon reads as grown from the sleeve | major | [I, from S hampton-gesture on straights] |
| GR-N05 | **Never** run the haft horizontally through the pelvis in an attack; it passes above or below it | cuts the body in half | major | [I; figure.md round-4 violation 12] |
| GR-N06 | **Never** ship a grip where the two hands can't be told apart at 3x | the grip spacing and the move can't be read | major | [I; CRITIQUE-PARAMS 8] |
| GR-N07 | **Never** let a sleeve or the body hide the grip and the haft in a rest pose (≥ 85% of the butt-to-disc line shows weapon or hand). *Added in round G1* | the weapon floats: the hand and half the haft vanish behind the bell | major | [M round G1: a haft 0.2 m behind her body plane put the near bell in front of the fist] |
