## 5. Cloth and outfit reads (CL)

**Principles.** Folds come from tension points where cloth anchors, tugs or meets resistance.
Pipe folds hang from a line of tension points; diaper folds hang between exactly two. Folds wrap
the form underneath. [S hampton-folds] Overlapping parts need colour contrast to read; on a
white costume that means value. [S azzi-pixel-logic secondary]

| ID | Parameter | Target | Sev | Basis |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| CL-P01 | Tension points | every fold starts at one: collar, armbands, hip band, garter ring, knees. The detached sleeve is a diaper fold between armband and wrist | minor | [S hampton-folds; I for her points] |
| CL-P02 | Tabard | hangs from the *tilted* hip band, off-centre toward the low hip; clings to the weight-leg thigh and swings off the free knee | major | [S hampton-folds; I] |
| CL-P03 | White-on-white overlaps | where one white part overlaps another, the back part steps down ≥ 2 ramp steps (W1 → W3), **or** a 1 px W4 contact line sits where they meet, **or** a dark accent runs between them | block | [S azzi-pixel-logic secondary; M W1/W2 = 1.30:1, W1/W3 = 1.94:1] |
| CL-P04 | Legs | thigh-highs are the dark indigo ramp (DESIGN 3 row 11), so a dark value always sits between the legs and under the tabard; the boot separates from the stocking by a gold top line and a darker core | block | [Dex; M W1/I2 = 6.84:1, I2/I4 = 1.95:1] |
| CL-P05 | Collar cross | visible in every front and three-quarter key pose: 5×5 px at 144 with a dark backing (DESIGN 3 row 1); no hand, hair lock or haft covers it; the free hand at the collar frames it rather than hiding it | block | [Dex; M G1/W2 = 1.13:1, so gold alone vanishes on white] |
| CL-P06 | Trumpet sleeves at size | at 144 the detached sleeve runs about 42 px from the armband to a 27 px mouth (DESIGN 2 at 96 × 1.5); it covers the elbow; the lower edge hangs by gravity from the elbow to the mouth; the indigo lining shows inside the mouth; the gold hem runs on the rim; pipe folds start at the elbow or armband (the tension points) and are ≥ 6 px long and 2 px wide below the pinch. *Added in round R1* | major | [DESIGN 2, 3 row 5; M 3D reference layer r4fix px144: near bell about 20 × 27 px; M C1 drew a 13 px drop; S hampton-folds] |
| CL-P07 | Gold trim on light cloth | every 1 px outfit gold pixel (in no 2×2 gold block) is one tone that separates from each light neighbour at ≥ 1.5:1: G2 beside W1/W2/S2, G3 beside W3/S3, G1 or G2 on the dark stockings; a run holds its tone; a G0 glint only alone. *Added in outfit art round 1* | major | [S imonk, jansson; M outfit round 1: 23–49% of gold\|white border pairs under 1.5:1 on the r0 stills] |
| CL-P08 | Tabard at size and value | at 144 the tabard is ≥ 9 px wide at the hem in front and three-quarter views (DESIGN 2: 11 × 51), a flat centre carrying the cross, folds only from the hip band; its back (lining) is never within 1.5:1 of the thigh-highs where it shows between the legs. *Added in outfit art round 1* | major | [DESIGN 2, 3 row 6; M outfit round 1: r0 hem 6–7 px at 144, a gold-noise strip at 80; the indigo lining merged with the I2 stockings in the back view] |
| CL-P09 | Chest window | the diamond sits on the centre line directly under the collar cross: a 1 px G2 frame around skin in two tones (S2 on the side facing the key light, S3 on the other) split by a 1 px S4 cleavage from a quarter of the way down, no gold inside; 7 × 12 at 144 (DESIGN 3 row 2's 5 × 9 at 96, × 1.5), 3 × 3 at 80 (a gold chevron over skin); built on the torso's own axis when it leans or foreshortens, never upright rows across a tilted chest. *Added in outfit art round 2.* *Changed in round O3:* the window reads as cleavage, not a frame: a Y-fork (S3) opening into a 1 px S4 line from a third of the way down, a lit S1 pixel on each inner curve beside it, S2 between, S3 under the curves, the frame closing at 10 rows and the line running on 2 px past the tip (S4, then a W4 cloth valley); it stands **upright**, moved sideways so its lower half sits on the sternum (was: directly under the cross, and a kite on the torso axis in lean poses: a 7 px diamond rotated by nearest-neighbour broke its frame into a leaf or a 'q', and the kite read as a gold sliver in Q); a chest foreshortened under 75% takes a 5 × 6 short stamp, never a narrowed one | major | [DESIGN 3 row 2; M outfit round 2: the critique's 5 × 9 at 144 left 3 px of skin and read as a gold drop pendant at x8 and 1x; at 80 a 5 × 6 read as a gold bar] |
| CL-P10 | Stocking top and garters | under the gold thigh band the stocking has a 1 px I1 welt; over it the skin has a 1 px S3 contact row (the squeeze); at most 2 straight straps per thigh, from the hip band to the thigh band, each ending in one 1 px clip; the front straps run on the front of the thigh, never the inner thigh; the back straps cross each cheek diagonally. *Added in outfit art round 2* | major | [visible ref 08 (welt, strap tension); M outfit round 2: inner-thigh straps read as gold scribble across the crotch in N1 and Q, vertical back straps as a box] |
| CL-P11 | Stocking against boot | two materials: the stocking matte (I3 shadow, I2 base, a thin I1 core, no streak), the boot one step darker (I4 shadow, I3 base) with one sharp I0 streak. The stocking's I3 threshold stays under the ambient term (v = 0.16 × ao on the unlit side). *Added in outfit art round 2.* *Changed in round O3:* the stocking is the dark K ramp (K1 core, K2 shadow from 0.22, K3 contact) with one continuous 1 px I1 sheen line from over the knee to the boot cuff at 144 (a 3 px knee glint at 80), 2 px inside the leg's edges and placed by N·H; the boot is near-black (K3 core, I4 only on the top-lit planes) with a short I0 streak down the shaft and one I0 toe glint: median luminance stocking / boot ≥ 1.3 (was: stocking I2/I3 against an I3/I4 boot, 1.0-1.2:1 on the sheets, so at 80 the leg read as one long boot) | minor | [critique 13; M outfit round 2: a threshold at 0.16 put 29% of the stocking in I4] |
| CL-P12 | Collar cross at the throat | the cross (5 × 5 on its 7 × 7 dark backing at 144) sits on the stand collar's front, on the neck's centre line, never hung in front of the bust; the collar in front is 7-8 px tall at 144 with a 1 px gold rim, and the chin/neck skin directly over it is one step darker (a 1 px shadow) so it reads raised. The chest window starts under the collar's foot, a separate shape. *Added in outfit art round 2b* | major | [DESIGN 3 row 1; critique 13a; M outfit round 2b: the pendant hung 25.6 mm in front of the bust projected 6-7 px right of and below the throat in the idle] |
| CL-P13 | Thong string | in any back view the back strap is one continuous 1 px OL line from the back medallion down the cleft to the glute folds' level, no gold on it and no wider than 1 px in any row; below the folds the gap shows what is behind. The glutes either side are shaded as forms (lit toward the key light, a shadow crescent underneath, a 1 px S4 fold from the cleft outward). *Added in outfit art round 2b* | major | [DESIGN rev 5 item 5, 3 row 10; critique 12b; M outfit round 2b `string`, `glute`] |
| CL-P14 | Tabard cross | the tabard's fleur-cross is DESIGN's 5 × 7 at 144 (3 × 5 at 80): 1 px G2 strokes on the white, G3 on the shade side of the flares, a G4 centre pixel; drawn only when the panel's front faces us (facing ≥ 0.25) and stands within 24° of upright, never inverted. *Added in outfit art round 2b.* *Changed in round O3:* the cross is at the round-1 control's weight: a 2 px stem (G2 lit column, G3 shade column), a 6 px arm, flared head and foot, 6 × 8 at 144, with G4 in the four inner corners of the crossing; at 80 a 3 × 3 plus in a G4-cornered 3 × 3 block (was: 5 × 7 with 1 px strokes, which read as a thin 'I' at 144 and vanished at 80; a full G4 plate round it filled the 7 px panel and read as a brown blob) | minor | [DESIGN 3 row 6; iconography rule; critique 13c: the 18 cm 3D cross rendered a blobby 7 × 10 gold mass] |
| CL-P15 | Gold budget at 80 | at 80 px the gold is the collar cross, the window frame, the hip band, the stocking top bands, the boot cuffs, the sleeve hems and the tabard's hem and cross only: the garter straps are dropped (the skin beside them fills in, their S4 border lines go too), the back harness over the hip band is one G3 line, the tabard's long side borders take its edge shade (W3 lit side, W4 shadow side); gold ≤ 14% of the rendered pixels in the hip-to-thigh box (sleeves, glaive and outline ring left out). *Added in outfit art round O3* | major | [critique 13e: 'at 80 it breaks into speckle across the hips and thighs'; M round O3: R2Q 17-30%, R3K3 9-15%] |
| CL-P16 | Garter straps at 144 | a strap is one 8-connected 1 px gold line (the render's stair-step doubles thinned away) and lies flush on the skin: no S4 inner line either side of it (the strap and its two S4 borders read as a 3 px dark 'inner-thigh outline'); the front pair on the front of the thigh (routes 57° → 40°), the back pair one per cheek. *Added in outfit art round O3* | minor | [critique 12b, 12c; M round O3: 32-51 border px per still] |
| CL-P17 | White cloth planes | the bust is a form on the bodice: per breast (the bodice's white between the sternum line and the silhouette, under the collar to the underbust anchor) W1 on the lit half as one cluster, a W2 ring, W3 on the turn and a 1 px W4 crescent along the underside; the detached sleeves at 144 carry three planes (W1 55%, W2 20%, W3) from the render's normals, 3 × 3 majority-cleaned; the tabard gets two fold lines (W3, the last 2 px W2) from the hip band toward the V-notch, converging, 55% and 40% of the way down, and a 1 px W3 shadow edge on the side away from the key light; ≤ 10% of the bodice's and tabard's horizontal one-tone runs longer than 6 px. B1 is never a white mid (it reads as skin beside the window). *Added in outfit art round O3* | minor | [critique 13g; M round O3: runs > 6 px 6-24% on R2Q, 0-8% on R3K3; visible x12: B1 mid read as skin] |
| CL-P18 | Back cross and open back | in a back view the hair tail and back mass are keyed off the spine (pose patch `hair4`), so the upper back and the halter read; the 3 × 5 yoke cross is a stamp on anchors (`ykx`), drawn only where the yoke is clear of hair and veil. *Added in outfit art round O3* | minor | [critique 12c; M round O3: the veil still covers the yoke anchor in N2, so the stamp does not fire (O-24)] |
| CL-P19 | Chest window, measured | the measure for CL-P09: in the window's box under the collar cross, an S4 column run ≥ 4 px and ≥ 2 S1 px at 144 (≥ 1 S4 px at 80), the box ≥ 5 px wide at 144 (≥ 3 at 80), so it never reads as a sliver or a plain gold frame. *Added in outfit art round O3* | major | [critique 12a: 'no cleavage line and no inner breast curves ... in Q it tilts into a thin gold sliver'; M round O3: R2Q 3/6, R3K3 6/6] |

| ID | Never / avoid | Why | Sev | Basis |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| CL-N01 | **Never** let cloth hang straight through a tilted pelvis | the hip band follows the pelvis; a straight tabard is a flat strip in front of the legs | major | [S hampton-folds] |
| CL-N02 | **Avoid** hair masses filling the neck and shoulder gap on both sides; keep ≥ 2 px of neck or shoulder visible on one side | the head sits on a column | major | [I] |
| CL-N03 | **Never** hang a CL-P06 bell from a forearm that crosses in front of the torso in an idle or hero still (the free hand at the collar): move the hand (the far hip) or pin the bell back | the drape fills the arm window (FG-N04) and covers the chest window and the hip band. *Added in round R1* | major | [M round R1: thumbnails t1, t3, t6 fail FG-N04 with the DESIGN-size bell; C1 passed only with a 13 px sleeve] |
| CL-N04 | **Never** leave the crotch uncovered at the centre of a splayed pose, or crossed by gold lines | the frame reads vulgar, not priestly; the tabard is keyed to fall between the thighs (a drape key, as its lifts are keyed) and the gold there stops at the hip band. *Added in outfit art round 2* | major | [critique 12 (Q): the most vulgar frame in the set; M outfit round 2 `qtab`] |
| CL-N05 | **Avoid** the front tabard in the leg gap of a back view | a light strip there erases the dark gap and a dark one reads as a third leg; its drape swings it clear (behind or beside the far leg) and its back is the dark lining with a dark rim. *Added in outfit art round 2* | major | [critique 12 (back); M outfit round 1 (lining = third leg), round 2 (`tabd`, `tabn2`)] |
| CL-N06 | **Never** let a flag read hollow: a tabard seen nearly edge-on in a motion frame (facing under 0.35) with its gold at 60% or more of its white is refilled as one solid white flag, gold on its lower edge only | two gold borders round a W3 strip read as a gold-outline ghost, not cloth | major | [critique 13c (N1); M outfit round 2b `tabsolid`: N1's tabard was G3 78 / W3 77 px] |
| CL-P20 | Cloth mass, measured | the idle hero's black-fill body area ÷ H² (glaive out, `finish_metrics.py`'s definition) is ≥ 0.26 at 144 and 80, carried by cloth and hair that stand **outside** the leg silhouette (bells, a flared tabard edge beside a leg, hair or veil past the skull), while the torso band's skin (0.18-0.52 H) keeps ≥ 0.85 of the pre-change count and the stockings ≥ 0.95 (the chest window, sides, open back and legs still show). Refs 07/08/09 measure 0.28-0.35, 04 0.17, so this is a costume lever, not a figure rule. *Added in route F3* | minor | [M finish-gap.md 2.5; M route F3: control 0.229 → L 0.265 (idle 144), 0.244 → 0.280 (80), skin band 1.08, stockings 0.99; `review/rosace/art/finish/F3/metrics.json`] |
| CL-P21 | The front garment covers the crotch | in every appeal idle, the front tabard hangs over the pelvis (no side wind on the tabard chain) and is at least hip-wide at the waist, flaring toward the hem. At 144 and 80 the crotch is never one skin panel from the navel to the thigh-tops; the 1 px thong string alone does not break it. *Added in round D9-R2* | major | [critics D9-R1 first impression and body: "the pelvis is one block of skin with a thin gold ribbon, so she reads bottomless, not elegant"; DESIGN's part table: the front tabard "covers the front"; M D9-R2: tabard x1.3 at the waist and x1.65 at the hem, g 1.0, idle mass 0.264 → 0.273] |
| CL-P22 | A signature head piece that reads at 80 | a gold front diadem on the hair's own outer contour at the brow, with the front lower than the back, on a 160° arc whose ends sink into the hair, and a jewel at the centre. The band is at least 0.03 m tall (2+ px at 144), so it draws as gold and not as outline only. Never a full ring: on the hair's outer volume a ring reads as a hat brim. Added parts carry the toon materials' `ao` attribute (1.0), or they render at the ramp's darkest stop. *Added in round D9-R2* | minor | [critic D9-R1 head and hair: "no signature priestess head piece; the side gold beads are tiny"; M D9-R2 trials t1-t2: a 0.02 m band drew as a dark line, the full ring read as a beret brim, and a mesh without `ao` rendered at v 0] |
| CL-P23 | Stocking and boot never share a ramp | the thigh-high is sheer: its lit side lets a muted rose through (mixed in by the light, up to 50%), while the boot is opaque, darker and bluer, with a stronger gloss, so the leg reads as skin, stocking and boot, not one navy tube. *Added in round D9-R3* | major | [critic D9-R2 outfit: "boots and thigh-highs are the same navy so the leg is one tube"; critic materials: "stockings read as opaque navy boots, no sheer skin-through tint like refs E/G"; `r3_finish.json` stocking `sheer`, boot ramp] |
| CL-N07 | **Never** add cloth mass inside the leg gap: a longer or wider front tabard that fills the λ of a wide stance in the three-quarter idle, a hem split into tails that point down between the legs, or a long tabard in the back view's leg gap | the black fill loses the λ; gold-edged tails read as spikes and extra legs; the back view reads a third leg (CL-N05). Length and width that the drape can't swing beside a leg don't count as mass. *Added in route F3* | major | [M route F3: A (tabard 0.80 / 0.128) filled the idle's leg gap; C and D (two tails from 55%) read as 4 gold lines and two extra legs at x3 and in the silhouette; E-G's back tabard fixed only by a drape to the free-leg side] |
| CL-N08 | **Never** let a bell's lining face the camera over the bust or chest | at ref 14's bell size the lining is a dark mass as big as the torso; flung toward the camera (N1's g 0.25 drape) it blacks out the chest window and the bust curve (DESIGN 3.5's never-list). Drape a big bell so it hangs or trails behind the far side in any still that shows the chest; its lining shows as a crescent at the mouth, not as a plane. *Added in route F3* | major | [M route F3: E-G N1 144, the far bell's lining over the whole chest; K (trail back, g 0.55) still over it; L (hangs, g 0.9) clears the bust, torso-band skin 0.87 of the control] |

---

## 6. Face (FC)

### 6.1 Principles

- **Head construction:** a ball for the cranium, a boxy jaw, flat side planes; the brow line
  runs through the middle of the side-plane oval; tilt and turn come from the ball. [S
  proko-head, after Loomis]
- **Anime placement:** the top of the eyes at or just under half the head's height; eyes about
  one eye-width apart (front); the nose about halfway from the eyes to the chin; the mouth a
  little above halfway from the nose to the chin. [S maryli-front, ao-face] Big eyes, little
  mouth, an almost non-existent nose. [S slynyrd-29]
- **Three-quarter view:** the centre line becomes a curve about ¾ across; the far eye loses
  width (the sources disagree on whether it loses height); the far brow is shorter; the nose
  moves toward the far eye; the mouth sits a little nearer the middle than the nose. [S
  maryli-34, ao-female34, csp-konart]
- **Eyes:** both irises point the same way; iris dark on top, light at the bottom; highlights
  sit on the cornea, where the iris meets the white, and the iris takes shadow, not highlight;
  outer lid ends angled down read strong, up reads soft, flat reads bored or tired. [S csp-eyes,
  csp-konart]
- **Expressions** live in three zones: brows, eyes and lids, mouth. A real smile lifts the lower
  lid and cheeks too; the change needed can be very slight. [S faigin, secondary]
- **Appeal:** no twinning; asymmetry is where the life is; big eyes and a small nose and mouth
  raise cuteness, but pushing every dial reads as a child. [S thomas-johnston, riki-twinning,
  glocker2009]
- **Charisma** [Dex]: her resting face is bright, confident and aimed at something. The critics'
  "blank half-lidded stare" is the face this rulebook exists to replace.

### 6.2 Construction grid at 144 (three-quarter view, facing screen-right)

Rows count up from the chin (row 0), because the chin sits on the collar and is the fixed point.
Ref measurements are in face.md 2.1.

| ID | Line or part | Target | Sev | Basis |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| FC-P01 | Vertical grid | chin 0; mouth 2–3; nose mark 6–7; eye bottom 9–11 (target 10); lash top 13–15 (target 14); brow at lash top +2 to +3; hair top 29–30 | block | [M refs 07/08/09; S maryli-front, ao-face] |
| FC-P02 | Face shape | face width at the eye row 15–16 px; (lash top to chin) ÷ width = 0.85–0.95 (three-quarter; front is checked at 0.75–1.0; a profile has no width across the eye row and is carried by FC-P01 and FC-P23, round R1) | block | [M refs 0.87–0.91, ours 0.69] |
| FC-P03 | Centre line (3/4) | a curve about ¾ across the face toward the far side; at eye level 1 px inside the far eye's inner corner | major | [S maryli-34; I px] |
| FC-P04 | Eye widths (3/4) | near eye 5–6 px wide; far eye 1–2 px narrower (4–5), 0–1 px shorter | major | [M refs near 5–7, far 5–6; S maryli-34, ao-female34; PIPELINE 3.2] |
| FC-P05 | Gap between the eyes | 3–4 px in 3/4; 5–6 px (one eye width) in front view | major | [M refs 3–4; S maryli-front] |
| FC-P06 | Nose | 1 px (front) or a 1 × 2 vertical mark (3/4), S3, 3–4 rows under the eye bottom, on the centre line, 1 px toward the far side | minor | [S maryli-34, slynyrd-29; M] |
| FC-P07 | Mouth | closed: 2–4 px of S4 line (an S3 tip or dimple outside the line is allowed and is not counted in its width); the **Confident mouth is 4 px of S4 with a corner above the lip line**: the round-3 pick is a shallow U (both corners up) with an S3 dimple at the near corner; open: 2 × 2 or 3 × 2; centred 0–1 px nearer the middle than the nose; far half 1 px shorter. A profile mouth follows FC-P23 (2–3 px, the back corner up). *Changed in round R2: was "the raised corner in S3"; changed in round R3: was "a smirk of at least 3 px" (3 px on a diagonal read as a pout, critique 2d; the U beat the 4 px smirk in the WF-P11 pick)* | major | [S maryli-34, slynyrd-29; M refs; PIPELINE 3.2; M round R1 critique 2c; M round R2 critique 2d; M round R3 pick] |
| FC-P08 | Chin and jaw | chin point 2–4 px wide, 1–2 px toward the far side; far contour bulges 1 px over rows 8–10 (cheekbone) then angles in; jaw taper starts at the eye bottom row. The offset and the bulge are three-quarter construction: a front chin sits on the centre line (*scope made explicit in round R1*) | major | [S maryli-34; I px; visible on 07] |

### 6.3 The eye at 144

The near eye is a box 5–6 wide × 5 tall; the far eye is the same minus 1–2 columns.

| Row | Content |
|---|---|
| 0 | the flick: 2 px of OL up and out past the box at the outer end (confident: up; resolute: flat; soft: down). *Round R3: the flick is the lash's second row* |
| 1 | upper lash, OL, 5 px across the box; the inner corner may dip 1 px onto the next row (the almond). *Round R3: R2's near eye carried a third lash row that squeezed the top iris row to 1 px (critique 2a)* |
| 2 | iris top: **A2** (A2/OL 3.24:1), a full row (≥ 2 iris px), with the highlight (A5, 1 px) on its lit edge, one column beside the pupil's column, the same in both eyes (FC-P12). *Round R2: not I3 (1.52:1 against the lash) and not A1 (1.58:1): both read as a third lid row* |
| 3 | iris middle, A2/A3 with the I3 pupil on the gaze side; the white (W1 or W2, 1–2 px) on the side **opposite** the gaze |
| 4–5 | iris bottom: a 2 px **A4 crescent** (a darker upper area and a brighter lower area, csp-okids); lower lash 1–2 px (OL) at the outer lower corner, never a full row; on the smirk side it rises into the eye (FC-P24) |

| ID | Rule | Target | Sev | Basis |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| FC-P09 | Upper lash | 2 rows on the outer ⅔, with a flick; almond shape: heavy at the outer end, light at the inner end. *Round R3 measurement: counted from the eye's topmost OL (the flick included) down to the first full iris row; Confident has exactly 2 (the flick row and one lash row). R2's near eye measured "4 rows, flick True" and passed* | major | [M refs 2 rows; S csp-eyes; M round R2 critique 2a] |
| FC-P10 | Iris | 3 px wide near, 2–3 px far; always touches the upper lash (an iris with white above it reads as shock); 3 iris tones + 1 highlight; 5–6 colours per eye, all palette | major | [S csp-eyes, csp-konart; M REF-BREAKDOWN] |
| FC-P11 | Gaze | both irises shift the same direction by the same amount. To look at the viewer from a head turned screen-right, both irises move 1 px screen-left and both whites sit screen-right. Every still names its target (viewer, enemy, blade) | block | [S csp-eyes; I for the px rule] |
| FC-P12 | Highlight | the same side in both eyes, on the key light's side (upper front, the side she faces: DESIGN 9); never mirrored; on the iris's lit upper edge, **both on the top full iris row and at the same offset from each pupil** (see open question O-3 on whether it may touch the white). *Changed in round R3: R2's catchlights sat at offsets (1, 0) and (1, −1) from their pupils, so the eyes focused on different points (critique 2b)* | major | [S csp-konart, aam-highlights; M round R2 critique 2b; I] |
| FC-P13 | Openness (charisma) | the resting face is open: at least 3 iris rows show under the lash (Confident, open Radiant, Ignited). Focused may drop to 2 rows because its lid is lowered on purpose. The half-lid (1–2 iris rows, soft flick) is reserved for the Serene expression. *Round R2: a row counts only if its iris tone stands at least 2:1 off the lash (OL); R1's I3 top row counted but read as lid. Round R3: and only with ≥ 2 iris px on the row; R2's single A2 px between lash pixels counted as a row* | block | [Dex; M round R1 critique 2a; M round R2 critique] |
| FC-P26 | Near eye dominance (3/4) | the near eye shows at least 1 more full iris row and 3 more iris-and-white px than the far eye, and at least as many bright px (A3, A4, A5, W), judged at 1x value, not box size. The R3 Confident: near 4 rows / 13 px, far 3 / 10. *Added in round R3* | major | [M round R2 critique 2a: R2's far eye showed 11 iris + 2 white px, the near 9 + 1; S maryli-34, ao-female34] |
| FC-P14 | Pupil | optional; if used, 1 px of the darkest iris tone in row 3–4, in the gaze direction; normal size on calm and confident faces (shrunk only for shock) | minor | [S csp-eyes] |

### 6.4 Brows, skin, blush, outline

| ID | Part | Target | Sev | Basis |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| FC-P15 | Brows | 3–4 px, 1 px thick, 2–3 rows above the lash top; neutral has a 1 px arch peaking over the outer third; far brow 1 px shorter and shifted outward. **Always present**, drawn over the bangs where the bangs cover the brow line; OL over hair, I3 on skin | block | [S maryli-34; M refs; PIPELINE 3.2] |
| FC-P16 | Skin | S2 lit plane; a **designed cast shadow under the fringe: at least 12 px of S3 in clusters of 2 px or more, hugging the clump undersides and shifted away from the key light**, never single pixels between tips; one under the chin on the neck (S4); an S1 2–4 px cluster on the lit cheek; one S3 cluster under the far cheekbone, slanted with the jaw. *Changed in round R3: was "one cast shadow under the bangs"; R2 passed with 7 px of checker specks (critique 2c, 2f)* | major | [S yu-mistakes, motomura-ggxrd, ao-shade-face; M round R2 critique 2f; I] |
| FC-P17 | Blush | 2–3 px of SB on **one row**, 1–2 rows under the eye bottom, under the outer half of each eye; far one 1 px shorter or equal; only on S2 (SB/S2 = 1.46:1), never on S3 (SB/S3 = 1.03:1, invisible). *Changed in round R2: was a diagonal hatch, which read as tear tracks beside the nose mark (FC-N24); a solid 2 px beat the p.p hatch in trial round b* | minor | [PIPELINE 3.2; M; M round R1 critique 2d] |
| FC-P27 | Brow windows | no hair px within 1 px (8 directions) of a brow px; on the open faces (Confident, Radiant, Serene) skin under every brow px. A frown may press the brow onto the lid (Focused, Ignited, Hurt). The fringe arches over each brow (a window) and its cast shadow sits above the brow, as on 07/08. *Added in round R3* | major | [M round R2 critique 2c: rows 18–20 alternated hair tips and skin, and the brows dissolved; S faigin (the lowered brow)] |
| FC-P28 | Placement on the v2 head | a face on the v2 (MMD) head is placed from the face pass (`author_faces_pass.py`: the head's own eye, brow and mouth meshes projected in this pose), one construction point per feature, never one stamp origin on the eye midpoint; each eye's origin sits on its projected iris centre, moved only by the logged anime cheats (eye-line roll clamped to 1 px, the near eye moved off the far one to keep the FC-P05 gap, the far eye pulled at most 2 px inside the silhouette). *Added in face round F1* | major | [M face round F1: the round-4 stamps on the v2 idle lost the mouth off the skin; S maryli-34 (the 3/4 cheat); Dex (human-authored head)] |
| FC-P29 | Brow windows are arches | a window cut into the fringe for a brow spans the brow +-1 px and the rows from one over the brow to the eye box; the clump between the eyes and the tips beside them stay (they frame the eyes like the refs' bangs). A window the width of the whole eye reads as a straight-cut fringe. *Added in face round F1* | major | [M face round F1 pick: full-width windows read as a headband at x3, narrow arches kept the centre clump; visible 07/08] |
| FC-P30 | Lower face on the v2 head | the lower face is rebuilt on the construction, not inherited from the render: the chin moved 2 px down the head's own axis (144; 1 px at 80), 1 px toward the far side of the start row's middle, each jaw side at least 1 px in from the render's by the chin row, a U taper from the mouth row into a 3 px chin; the neck px under the new jaw take a 1 px S3 shadow; below the eye line the face holds S2, blush and the features only (the render's S3/S4 flattened, its brown contour ring stepped to S3). A profile's front contour is skin: the nose a 1 px skin bump, the lips a 1 px SB notch, no brown or OL stack on the face silhouette. *Added in face round F2* | major | [M face round F2: idle FC-P02 0.73 -> 0.87, FC-P08, FC-N13, FC-N15 fail -> pass; critique F1 2c, 2d, 2g; S maryli-34] |
| FC-P31 | One eye template per view | per size and view (q34 near, q34 far, front, profile) one eye template; every expression is that template with other lids and brows, never another eye size. At one size and one view the open-eye iris height (rows carrying A2-A5 or the I3 pupil) differs by at most 1 px across the stills (144 q34: Confident 5 rows, Focused 4). *Added in face round F2b* | major | [M critique F1 2h (eye size, lash weight and brow style changed between idle, N1 and N2); M face round F2b: idle 5 / N1 4, near and far] |
| FC-P32 | Mouth on the centre line | every still that shows the face has a mouth: at least one S4/OL mouth px whose centre lies within 1 px of the face's centre line at the mouth row (the nose tip to the chin, 0.52 of the way down at 144, 0.55 at 80, from facepass). *Added in whole round WH2* | major | [M critique WH1 face (144 idle mouth lost, "a brown smudge off the centre line", 80 "the mouth dot drifts right"); M WH2 wh2.json checks] |
| FC-P33 | Open eyes on attack frames | an attack still keeps both eyes open, narrowed by the lid (the lid falling toward the nose for a fierce look), at least 3 iris px per eye at 144 and 2 at 80; closed ^^ or flat-line eyes are for non-attack beats only. Each pose's expression comes from one table (art/rosace/faces/wh2.json: idle smirk, N1 focused, Q fierce with an open mouth, back glance). *Added in whole round WH2* | major | [M critique WH1 face and appeal (Q's closed-eye grin "reads as silly more than beautiful")] |
| FC-P34 | Eyes fitted to the face window | each eye starts on its facepass centroid and may move at most 3 px across and 1 down or up (80: 1 and 1) so that most of it shows on the painted face (iris px count 3, lid px 1, 2 off per px moved), both eyes fitted together; the two eye stamps never touch (1 free px) and the irises stay 2 px apart (80: 1). The 80 px face has its own stamps (eye 2x3 with a lid row, mouth 1-2 px, no blush, no nose). *Added in whole round WH2* | major | [M WH2: on the raw centroids N1's two lids touched under the fringe edge (one dark bar with a blue slit) and Q's near eye lay under the side lock] |
| FC-P35 | A clean face under a painterly finish | the head's skin takes no brush texture and a lifted ramp input (0.3 + 0.7 v), so the terminator reaches only the far jaw and under the fringe; the blush is drawn soft (a core at about 55% blush over the local skin and a 1 px ring at about 22%), never two pink blocks. *Added in round D9* | major | [critic FJ1: "blush should be soft AA"; M D9: without the lift the textured skin ramp left salmon and terracotta specks on the near cheek at 144; refs' faces are 2-3 tones (finish-gap.md 2.4)] |
| FC-P36 | The painterly face spec, one face for every shot | at 144:<br>• an upper-lid wedge: a 1 px flick lifting at the outer corner, then two dark rows over the outer third tapering to one row and a soft I3 end at the inner corner;<br>• under it one A2 band across the iris top (the lid's shadow), then a 2x2 dark pupil with the catchlight beside it, a mid A3 and a light A4 crescent;<br>• the catchlight on the same side in both eyes;<br>• a lower lash of 1-2 px in a rose mid tone, never black;<br>• iris colours desaturated but light enough to read under the wedge (A3 about `#5f84c2`, never a dark navy iris);<br>• every shot takes the open eye; attack faces change the mouth (a set line on N1, a small symmetric open mouth on Q), never the lid (no lowered 'focus' or slanted 'fierce' lid);<br>• a 4 px closed smile with the camera-side corner up at idle and back (3 px at 80). *Added in round D9-R2; changed in round D9-R3* (old: a 4-tone iris under a one-row lid, the idle/back spec only) | major | [critic D9-R1 face: "big cyan eyes, a thick black lash bar, a 1-2 tone iris, a 1-2 px mouth; pleasant but blank"; `drive9/r2_faces.json`] |
| FC-P37 | The back glance takes a profile eye | when the far eye's 3D facing is under 0.3 (the facepass anchors), the face step draws one profile eye (the near one), never a two-eye front stamp on a head turned over the shoulder. The glance itself is a look amount of about 0.75 (a three-quarter face over the shoulder), not a full turn to the camera (1.0 read near-frontal) and not a profile turned away (0.62 read as a scowl away from the viewer). The turned face takes a fill (face lift about 0.55), never full shadow. *Added in round D9-R2; changed in round D9-R3* (old: look amount about 0.6) | major | [critic D9-R1: "neither is a back view: both show a near-frontal face"; critic D9-R2: "the back view's face turns into profile, away from the viewer ... full shadow ... reads as a scowl"; M D9-R2: look 1.0 → 0.62 took the head's facing from 0.78 to 0.44; trials D9-R3 0.85 (near-frontal) and 0.75 (kept)] |
| FC-P38 | Face form on a painterly face | the flat face skin gets its form from three authored steps, not from the render's terminator:<br>• the head's cast shadow on the neck: 2 rows under the chin (1 at 80) in a rose shadow (`#c9868a` at 70% then 35%);<br>• the jaw: face skin with no face skin under it takes one soft step toward the skin ramp's 0.42 tone;<br>• a blush drawn by the finish, 2 px under each eye's outer half (1 px at 80) with a 1 px soft ring, since a stamp blush under a rose lower lash disappears. *Added in round D9-R3* | major | [critic D9-R2 face: "the skin is one flat pale peach with no cheek or jaw form ... D has the blush, F lost it"; `drive9/r3_post.face_extras`; M D9-R3 idle 144: neck 2, jaw 21, blush 6 px] |
| FC-P18 | Face outline | no dark line inside the face; against the background the silhouette OL is the edge; against hair, the hair's dark tone is; a far-jaw line, if needed, is S3, never S4 | major | [visible refs; I] |

### 6.5 Expressions

Each expression changes at least two of brows, lids and mouth (FC-N19). The default is
**Confident** [Dex].

| Expression | Used for | Brows | Lids and eyes | Mouth | Head |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Confident** (default: idle, walk, menus) | resting face | near brow 3 px with its outer end a step up, in a skin window; far brow 2 px, level, under the fringe shadow (FC-N26, FC-P27) | open, the near eye the bigger (FC-P13, FC-P26); the lash = the flick + 1 row, the inner corner dipping 1 px (FC-P09, FC-N25); the smile side's lower lid lifted (FC-P24); irises at the viewer with an I3 pupil, catchlights on the top iris row (FC-P12) | a 4 px S4 smile, both corners up (a shallow U), an S3 dimple at the near corner (round R3; R2's 3 px smirk read as a pout) | tilted 1 px across the eye line (FC-P25) |
| **Focused** (attacks) | strikes, dashes | inner ends 1–2 px lower than the outer ends, 1 px closer together | upper lash flattened to a straight 2-row bar sitting 1 px lower on the iris; lower lid up 1 px (2 iris rows show); eyes on the target | pressed 2 px line, or 2 × 2 open with a W1 tooth pixel for a shout | chin down 1 px |
| **Radiant** (wins, happy) | victory, ultimate hold | raised 1 px, relaxed | closed arcs curving up in the middle, or open with the lower lid pushed up 1 px (a crescent) | corners up: a 3–4 px U, or open with SB | tilt 1–2 px |
| **Serene** (the only half-lid) | prayer beats: the staff-turn flourish, Sanctuary, R's cut-in wind-up | level, relaxed, 1 px higher than Focused | half-lidded: the lash lowered so 1–2 iris rows show, the flick soft (flat or down) | small closed smile, one corner up | tilted, chin slightly down |
| **Ignited** (R, Illumination) | power state | as Focused, pulled 1 px in toward the nose | the Focused lids; the iris keeps a **dark A2 core inside an A4 rim** (a pale fill reads blind), a 1 px A4 glow beside the eye | a battle cry: the teeth as one W1 row on the mouth line, the dark mouth (OL) under it, the lower lip (S4) under that: 3 × 3 (profile 2 × 2). *Round R3: R2's single W1 px over SB read as buck teeth (critique 2g; ao-teeth)* | – |
| **Hurt** | hit reactions | inner ends up 1–2 px (worry, allowed here) | squeezed into > < shapes pointing at the nose (a flat line read as asleep) | 2 px with one corner down, or a small open oval | pushed back |

| ID | Rule | Target | Sev | Basis |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| FC-P19 | Expression set | every expression above exists per facing and differs from Confident in at least two zones | major | [S faigin secondary; Dex] |
| FC-P20 | Asymmetry | every hero drawing has at least one asymmetry: head tilt (1 px eye-line tilt), brow height, mouth corner, or near/far eye width | major | [S thomas-johnston, riki-twinning; M refs, low confidence on the tilt] |
| FC-P21 | Eye line and fringe move together | any change of the eye row (a variant, a roll, a pitch) moves the bang tips over that eye by the same rows, so HR-P04 and FC-N17 still hold. *Added in round R1* | major | [M round R1: the one-axis eye_row variant broke FC-N17 in 12 of 12 faces; the tips stayed where the old eye line was] |
| FC-P22 | The eye's value design | on open eyes the iris top row, right under the lash, is **A2**; the middle A2/A3 with an optional I3 pupil on the gaze side; the bottom row carries a 2 px A4 crescent (3 iris tones + the A5 highlight on the lit edge); the white is one column beside the highlight, at most 2 rows, on the side opposite the gaze. Two or more white columns read startled on a 5–6 px eye. *Added in round R1; changed in round R2: the top was I3, which merged with the lash (1.52:1)* | major | [M round R1 round a; S csp-eyes (iris dark on top), csp-konart, csp-okids; M round R1 critique 2a] |
| FC-P23 | Profile construction | nose tip on row 6–7 (about a quarter of the head above the chin), bottom lip about row 3, the mouth a 2 px mark 1 px inside the contour; the eye box set back so its front is 2 px behind the front contour, 4 px wide with a 3 px iris; the lash flick sweeps back; the neck leans forward. *Added in round R1* | major | [S ao-side; S maryli-side (secondary, search excerpt) for the eye at the head's midline; I px] |
| FC-P24 | The smile reaches the eyes | on Confident (and an open Radiant) the lower lid on the smirk side lifts 1 px: that eye's bottom iris row is narrower than the row above it, with a lid mark (OL) rising from the outer lower corner. *Added in round R2* | major | [S proski-expr ("overlap the lower lid with the cheek"), faigin; M round R1 critique 2c/3] |
| FC-P25 | Confident head tilt | the Confident three-quarter face rolls the eye line 1 px: the far eye, its brow and its blush sit 1 px higher than the near ones (the head spec's `roll`). *Added in round R2* | major | [Dex / DESIGN 5; S thomas-johnston; M round R1 critique 3: both lash tops were on one row] |

### 6.6 Face negative rules

| ID | Never / avoid | Why | Sev | Basis |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| FC-N01 | **Never** put the whites on the nose side of both eyes | the irises splay outward and she looks at nothing: the "dead stare" | block | [S csp-eyes] |
| FC-N02 | **Never** flip a finished eye to make the other one; flip the shape if you like, then redo the highlight and iris offset | the light and the gaze end up pointing opposite ways | major | [S csp-eyes for the flip; I for the warning] |
| FC-N03 | **Never** make the far eye as wide as the near eye in 3/4 | a front face pasted on a turned skull | major | [S maryli-34] |
| FC-N04 | **Avoid** a white row above the iris on calm and confident faces | reads as shock | major | [S csp-eyes] |
| FC-N05 | **Never** put the highlight mid-iris, on the lash, or in opposite corners of the two eyes | the eye reads as floating or cross-lit | major | [S csp-konart] |
| FC-N06 | **Avoid** a 1-row upper lash at 144 | reads as a thin brow-like line | major | [M refs use 2 rows] |
| FC-N07 | **Never** let the lash touch I4 or OL hair without a skin or I1/I2 pixel between | OL on I4 is 1.25:1; the eye merges into the fringe | major | [M] |
| FC-N08 | **Avoid** a symmetric round-arch lash | an owl or surprised eye | minor | [I, visible 07–09] |
| FC-N09 | **Never** ship a face outside FC-P02's ratio | eyes too low and a short chin read squashed; too high reads older and heavy | block | [S maryli-front; M] |
| FC-N10 | **Avoid** centring the nose between the eyes in 3/4 | it goes toward the far eye | minor | [S maryli-34] |
| FC-N11 | **Never** draw a closed mouth wider than 4 px at 144 | a grimace or a cartoon; the mouth is the subdued feature | major | [S slynyrd-29; I px] |
| FC-N12 | **Avoid** a nose over 2 px, or in S4 or OL | pulls toward realism; looks older or dirty | minor | [S slynyrd-29; I px] |
| FC-N13 | **Never** run a straight vertical cheek contour longer than 3 rows below the eyes (either side), or a chin row that reads wider than 4 px (neck skin beside the chin counts) | the face becomes a box | major | [I; M ours 7 rows and 7 px; M round R1 critique 2e: a 3 px chin on a 6 px neck row read 9 px; round R2 measures both contours] |
| FC-N14 | **Never** put an S3 ring just inside the face edge | pillow shading | major | [S yu-mistakes] |
| FC-N15 | **Never** leave a stray pixel inside the face: every face pixel belongs to a feature or to a shading cluster of ≥ 2 px (8-connected from round R3, as PX-P12) | noise on the most-read part of the sprite | major | [S saint11-1] |
| FC-N16 | **Never** use S4 (the mouth colour) for contours inside the face | a second mouth or a beard shadow | major | [I; M round-4 attack face] |
| FC-N17 | **Never** let a hair lock cross an eye or run down the middle of the face; no hair pixel between the eyes below the lash top | a scar or tear streak; deletes the iris | block | [I; M round-4 attack face] |
| FC-N18 | **Never** ship a perfectly mirrored face on a hero drawing | twinning | major | [S thomas-johnston, riki-twinning] |
| FC-N19 | **Never** change only one zone between expressions | the same blank face with a different mouth | major | [S faigin secondary; I] |
| FC-N20 | **Never** pair a big smile with neutral eyes | reads as fake | major | [S faigin secondary] |
| FC-N21 | **Never** ship a still whose gaze has no named target | the blank stare | block | [I] |
| FC-N22 | **Never** use the half-lid as the default face; it's the Serene expression only | the critics' "no charisma" read | block | [Dex] |
| FC-N23 | **Never** omit the brows | a face without brows has no mood | block | [PIPELINE 3.2; M round-4 idle] |
| FC-N24 | **Never** put two vertical 1 × 2 marks (S3, S4, SB, I3) on the same rows between the eye bottom and the mouth; the nose is the only vertical mark. *Added in round R2* | they read as tear streaks and the face reads sad | major | [M round R1 critique 2d; I] |
| FC-N25 | **Never** let the lash slope down toward the outer corner on Confident or an open Radiant: the outer end sits at or above the middle of the lash and a flick rises up and out past the box; the lid line may fall toward the nose. *Added in round R2* | down at the outside reads tired | major | [S csp-yitsuin (the angle of the lid line carries the character; down toward the outside reads tired), csp-okids (triangular outer ends); M round R1 critique 2a] |
| FC-N26 | **Never** raise the inner end of a brow above its outer end on Confident, Focused or Ignited. *Added in round R2* | the worry brow: it made R1's Confident face read sad | major | [S faigin; M round R1 critique 2b] |
| FC-N27 | **Never** speckle the fringe edge: between the hairline and the lash top, no row of the face span holds more than 2 single-pixel runs of hair or S3 (runs touching the span's ends are the framing clumps and do not count). *Added in round R3* | the checker of I3 tips and S2 skin that dissolved R2's brows into a unibrow | major | [M round R2 critique 2c; S saint11-2, slynyrd-57] |
| FC-N28 | **Never** draw a jaw contour as a staircase of 4 or more equal steps; the runs shorten toward the chin as a curve's do (the q34 far jaw: 4, 3, 2, 1, 1). *Added in round R3* | equal steps are a straight line: R2's 1:1 jaws made a guitar-pick face | major | [M round R2 critique 2e; S lospec-outlines ("5, 2, 2, 1, 1" not "5, 2, 1, 2, 1")] |
| FC-N29 | **Never** leave a hair strand crossing the face below the brow band: inside the face window (the head skin alone, face pass) no run of 1-3 hair px with skin on both sides. Framing locks on the window edge (2 px) stay. *Added in face round F1* | a thin lock across the cheek reads as a scar or a tear streak; the v2 hair mesh puts 2-10 such px on the key stills | major | [M face round F1: N1 10, back view 9, idle 2 px on the round-4 stills; I] |
| FC-N30 | **Never** leave a hair px inside an eye's body (its lash row down to its lid) or on a cheek (below the lowest eye body, inside the face window's span, off its edge px); and never carve a skin window through the fringe to make room for a brow or a lash: the fringe silhouette stays, its flat bottom runs taper to 1 px tips, and a brow goes on where the fringe already shows skin or as a 1 px OL stroke over the fringe clumps inside the head-skin mask (FC-P15; nothing is cut). *Added in face round F2; changed in face round F2b: was "a brow goes on only where the fringe already shows skin" (on every v2 still that meant no brows, and FC-P15/FC-N23, both block, failed by design)* | a lock across the eye deletes the iris; a carved window reads as a chopped fringe or a receding hairline | major | [M critique F1 2b, 2f (the F1 windows read as a headband and chopped bangs); M face round F2: 0 eye / 0-1 cheek px on the eight stills] |
| FC-N31 | **Never** let a lash, flick or brow px leave the face: every OL px the face adds above the mouth row lies inside the head-skin mask (the face pass's facewin) grown by 1 px and, on its row, inside the visible face contour (the span of window skin on that row and the 4 under it) grown by 1 px. *Added in face round F2b* | a flick lying on the side hair reads as the lash sliding off the head | major | [M critique F1 2a (idle's near lash ran past the contour onto the hair); M face round F2b: F2 pass 1 idle 1 px, Q 1 px, F2b 0 on all eight stills] |

## 7. Hair (HR)

**Principles.** Build the skull first, mark the hairline, then the hair as a few big clumps that
stand off the skull; splits between clumps don't go up past the hairline. In three-quarter view
the middle clumps point down and the side clumps swing toward their own side. [S ao-hair34]
Imagine the flow lines of the main locks from a cowlick at the back, blob the hair in one
colour, then add shadow and highlight. [S slynyrd-29] Hair is too small for strands at this
size: draw ribbon-like clumps that follow the skull and fall with gravity; darker sends a clump
back, lighter brings it forward. [S kawaiihannah, from its search excerpt]

| ID | Parameter | Target at 144 | Sev | Basis |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| HR-P01 | Order | skull → hairline → flow lines from the crown or parting → clump blobs in one tone → shadow → highlight | major | [S ao-hair34, slynyrd-29] |
| HR-P02 | Volume | hair top 2–3 px above the skull ball; not a cap on the skull line | minor | [S ao-hair34; I px] |
| HR-P03 | Bangs | 3–5 clumps, 3–5 px wide at the root, tapering to 1–2 px tips, starting from a parting or the crown | major | [S ao-hair34; I px; DESIGN 6] |
| HR-P04 | Bang tips | over each eye the fringe stops above the brow window (never in the eye box, FC-N17; never within 1 px of a brow, FC-P27); a clump beside each eye (within 2 columns of the box) frames it, reaching to within 2 rows of that eye's lash top; skin shows above each eye. *Changed in round C1 (was: "end on the lash row or 1–2 rows into it"); changed in round R3 (was: "over each eye, end 1–2 rows above that eye's own lash top"): a tip over the eye 1–2 rows above the lash lands on the brow, which is what dissolved R2's brows (critique 2c)* | major | [I, visible 07–09; M round C1; M round R2 critique 2c] |
| HR-P05 | Sidelocks | may cover the near jaw edge; leave the far cheek and chin contour visible in 3/4 | minor | [S maryli-34; I] |
| HR-P06 | Separators | clump splits one step darker than the clump's shadow, not OL | minor | [M REF-BREAKDOWN 01; DESIGN 6] |
| HR-P07 | Tones | I1 lit, I2 mid, I3 shadow; I4 only for clump lines and deep undersides; at least a third of hair pixels in I1–I2 | major | [M I4/OL = 1.25:1; DESIGN 4] |
| HR-P08 | Highlight | an angel ring broken into one dash per clump, **the dashes on at least 3 different rows following the skull's curve** (lower at the back and the front, highest over the crown), within a 4-row band, each tapering from I0 into I1 along its clump's flow. *Changed in round R3: R2's dashes sat on one row, a halo band (critique 4a)* | minor | [S kawaiihannah, slynyrd-29, ao-shade-hair; M round R2 critique 4a; I] |
| HR-P09 | Tips | shift to A3 then A4 over the last 2–4 px (no A2 step: A2 against I1/I2 hair is under 1.5:1, PX-P08; round R1) | minor | [DESIGN 6; M REF-BREAKDOWN 03; M round R1] |
| HR-P10 | Bang clumps split | at least one of the 3–5 root clumps of HR-P03 splits into two sub-clumps under the roots, with tips of different lengths (one per eye ending 1–2 rows over the lash top per HR-P04, the other shorter); the fringe shadow under them is a designed band, not specks between the tips. *Added in round R1; changed in round R2: was "each root clump splits into 1–2 px sub-clumps", which made the comb* | minor | [S ao-hair34 (big clumps first, then split); M round R1 round b: bangs_split kept; visible 07/08 thin pointed tips] |
| HR-P11 | Unequal fringe | root clumps of different widths from a parting (the q34 fringe is 4 / 6 / 5 / 3 at its split row), one lock may sweep across it; the lower edge of the fringe takes at least 3 tip rows across the face and never repeats a pattern of period 6 px or less three times, in its tip rows or in its tones; the brows sit in designed forehead windows between clumps. *Added in round R2* | major | [S skyrye-hair ("mix one or two large pieces with smaller cuts"), ao-hair34; M round R1 critique 4: a tone pattern repeated on a 3 px period] |
| HR-P13 | Silhouette break | at least one clump tip breaks the head silhouette, drawn in the hair's lit or mid tones (I0–I2) with a root of 2 px or more; the dome itself stays inside FC-P01 (no crown cowlick). *Added in round R3* | minor | [M round R2 critique 4b; M round R3 trial d (temple_flick kept; cowlick failed FC-P01); S skyrye-hair] |

The occlusion line never draws a black bar across the brow. *Added in round D9-R2* | major | [critic D9-R1 face: "almost no fringe shadow or anti-aliasing into the hair"; the "straight dark brows" were the fringe's occlusion line; M D9-R2 idle 144: 6 + 5 fringe-shadow px, 30 AA px] |
| HR-P25 | Hair clump separators and hue shift | in a painterly chain, where two modelled clump groups (hair part ids) meet inside the hair mass, the darker pixel of the pair takes a dark tone from the hair's own ramp (rank 0.15, at 70%); runs under 2 px are dropped. The hair ramp shifts toward indigo in the shadows and toward pink-lavender in the lights. *Added in round D9-R2* | minor | [critic D9-R1 head and hair: "one purple curtain ... hue-shift the shadows toward indigo or teal and the highlights toward pink-lavender"; M D9-R2: 145-187 separator px at 144] |
| HR-P26 | Authored hair reads on a rendered head | in a painterly chain the hair's signature reads are drawn from the render's own masks, not from its spec band:<br>• the sheen is an arc a fixed share (0.3) of the crown-to-eye height under the hair's own top edge, sinking 2 px toward the sides (a ring seen from a little above), cut into 3-5 px segments with 1 px gaps and a 1 px zigzag, core colour on the key-lit 60% and halo on the rest;<br>• the fringe's bottom edge is cut at 4 unequal places (2 at 80) from an off-centre parting: a forehead-shadow notch under each gap and a 2 px dark separator above it;<br>• the azure tips are a 3-step taper by distance from the hair, with the hair's last pixel stepped toward deep azure;<br>• 2-3 flyaway strands (1 at 80) launched along the silhouette's downward tangent, never straight out of the crown (a strand launched outward reads as an antenna). *Added in round D9-R3* | minor | [critic D9-R2 head and hair: "a banded purple helmet with a single horizontal sheen stripe ... no strand breakup, flyaways, dark inter-clump gaps or tapered azure tips"; `drive9/r3_post.hair_r3`, `flyaways`; trial D9-R3: crown flyaways at 60° and 128° read as antennae] |

| ID | Never / avoid | Why | Sev | Basis |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| HR-N01 | **Never** draw the fringe as one helmet mass with notches | no clump reads with a root and a tip; no forehead window | major | [S ao-hair34; M round-4 idle] |
| HR-N02 | **Avoid** a speckled highlight (scattered I0/I1 pixels; from round R3 measured as I0/I1 px with no I0/I1 neighbour, so a dash's tapering tail is not a speck) | noise, not gloss | minor | [S saint11-2; M round 4] |
| HR-N03 | **Avoid** I4 as a mass tone | it merges with the outline | major | [M 1.25:1] |
| HR-N05 | **Never** let a dark (I3/I4) 1 px strand stick out of the head silhouette. *Added in round R3* | inside its outline it reads as a twig, a horn or an antenna (trial round d: flyaway, flyaway_curl; R1's crown antenna) | major | [M round R3 trial d; M round R1 trial a] |
| HR-N04 | **Never** draw straight parallel clump lines: an interior hair line (darker than both neighbours) bends 1 px at least every 3 rows. *Added in round R2* | parallel straights band (PX-N02) and read as a comb or stripes | major | [S ao-hair34; DESIGN 6 (S-curves); M round R1 critique 4] |
| HR-N06 | **Never** cut a skin island round a brow: a skin ring with hair under it, between the brow and the eye. *Added in round H2* | it reads as a third eye or an @ on the forehead | major | [M round H2 trial (face_window ring)] |
| HR-N07 | **Never** end a fringe strand inside the face in a light tip tone (A3/A4): a strand whose last px lies on face skin between the eyes or over the nose keeps its hair tone to the tip. *Added in the Integrate step* | a 2-3 px light-blue column between the eyes reads as a tear or a scar, not a hair tip | major | [M Integrate: N1 144, hair_px 'tips' on r2f's centre clump with the F2b face] |
| HR-N08 | **Never** leave dark 1-2 px specks inside the hair mass: an I3/I4 px with 3 or more lighter hair 4-neighbours, or an I3/I4 4-connected cluster of 1-2 px with 5+ hair neighbours per px. One dark tone (I4 folds into I3). *Added in whole round WH2* | render AO noise reads as dirt, not clumps | major | [M critique WH1 ("noisy speckled dark clusters ... like rendered AO noise"); M WH2 38-56 px per 144 still] |
| HR-N09 | **Never** sweep the hair tail into the idle silhouette where the back hair joining it to the head is hidden behind the body | the tail pokes out beside the hip as a separate ribbon, and its fan of azure tips reads as a claw. Hair mass in the idle comes from volume past the skull or a mass that visibly continues from the head. *Added in route F3* | major | [M route F3: E, G and H (tail g 0.45-0.55, wind to screen-left and back) at 144 x3 and x5: a ribbon from the hip with 3-5 azure prongs; the mass it bought (0.265 → 0.278) was not worth the read] |
