## 8. Pixel rendering (PX)

### 8.1 Principles

- **One light, committed before shading;** flat planes get one colour, curved forms a ramp;
  sharp terminators beat smooth gradients; pillow shading is the named failure. [S slynyrd-6-5,
  jansson, saint11-4, yu-mistakes, parmesan-aa]
- **Hue-shift every ramp** (≤ 20° per step); shadows cooler, lights warmer; big low-saturation
  areas, small saturated accents; few, distinct colours. [S slynyrd-1, saint11-6, yu-mistakes]
- **Clusters:** keep them few and big; orphans are noise; anything that must be shaded needs
  ≥ 2 px thickness. [S saint11-2, slynyrd-2, yu-mistakes]
- **Lines:** steady step rhythm, no doubles, interior lines lighter than the silhouette,
  sel-out on the lit side. [S saint11-2, jansson, rickyhan, mortmort, slynyrd-57]
- **AA** by hand, interior only, only on steps ≥ 2 px. **No dithering on animated characters.**
  [S saint11-5, parmesan-aa, parmesan-dither]
- **Don't look like a render:** Arc System Works hand-controlled where shadows fall on Guilty
  Gear Xrd's models and chose "expressiveness over accuracy". The render says where forms are;
  the artist decides the shadow shapes. [S motomura-ggxrd]

### 8.2 Parameters

| ID | Parameter | Target | Sev | Basis |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| PX-P01 | Key light | one per sprite, top-front, from the same screen side in every frame of a move | major | [S slynyrd-6-5, jansson] |
| PX-P02 | Terminator | one clean light-to-shadow edge per form; transition ≤ 1 px wide on forms under 12 px | major | [S saint11-4, jansson; I width] |
| PX-P03 | Cast shadows | designed shapes ≥ 2 px thick at the widest: under the bangs, under the chin, under the bust, where the tabard meets the thigh, under the sleeve cuff | major | [S motomura-ggxrd; I list] |
| PX-P04 | Tones per material | 3 (light, shadow, deep) plus a highlight only on glossy materials (hair, gold, steel, boots) | major | [M refs 3–5; S saint11-4] |
| PX-P05 | Hue shift | 5–20° per ramp step, cool in shadow, warm in light | minor | [S slynyrd-1, saint11-6] |
| PX-P06 | First shadow step on light materials | lit → first shadow ≥ 1.6:1 on white, skin and gold | major | [M ref 01 white 1.75, 07 skin 2.4; ours W 1.30, S 1.42] |
| PX-P07 | Tones that must separate at 1x | ≥ 1.3:1 | major | [I, PIPELINE 3.4] |
| PX-P08 | Different materials meeting | ≥ 1.5:1 across the border, or a line or shadow tone between | block | [M 45% of our gold/white border pairs are under 1.5; S jansson] |
| PX-P09 | Value groups | ≥ 3 separated groups (dark, mid, light), each ≥ 15% of the figure | major | [S steam-grey; M] |
| PX-P10 | Brightest tone share | ≤ 3% of the figure on mid or dark costumes; on white costumes ≤ 14% only if the next step down is ≥ 1.7:1 | major | [M refs 0.4–2.2%, ref 01 13.5%] |
| PX-P11 | Specular | 1–3 px clusters, ≤ 1% of the material, one per form, lit side | minor | [S jansson, imonk] |
| PX-P12 | Orphans | none inside a flat area of ≥ 12 px, except AA, the eye, a highlight or a deliberate texture pixel. An orphan has no neighbour of its colour in all 8 directions: a pixel on a 1:1 line is not one (*round R1: the check had been 4-connected and flagged every 45° line*) | major | [S saint11-2, jansson, slynyrd-2] |
| PX-P13 | Shade-shape thickness | every shadow shape on a limb or panel is ≥ 2 px thick somewhere; no 1 px shadow strip along an outline | major | [S yu-mistakes, parmesan-aa] |
| PX-P14 | Banding | ≤ 3.5 hugging run pairs per 1,000 figure px, not counting line parts at most 3 px thick (the haft), which PX-P21 judges instead (*round R1 answers O-7; was: every figure pixel*) | major | [M refs 2.0–3.4, ours 6.2–10.0; S azzi-pixel-logic secondary; M round R1: 43 of 67 pairs were the haft] |
| PX-P15 | Colour count | toon chain (the integrated stills): ≤ 32 on the sprite; 95% of pixels in ≤ 20. Painterly chain (a 3-4x render through PX-P42, drive 9): no total cap; the budget is per material, 5-10 ramp tones plus ≤ 3 in-betweens (AA, rim, bust shading) after the final requantise, lines and face stamps extra. *Changed in round D9; old value: "≤ 32 on the sprite; 95% of pixels in ≤ 20" for every chain* | major | [M; QUALITY-RUBRIC 6; finish-routes judges FJ1: "PX-P15's 32 and F1's 40 per group are both far below the refs"; M D9: 118-156 colours at 80/144 with 5-10 tones per material] |
| PX-P16 | Local density | ≤ 4 distinct tones in any 6×6 window, except the face and weapon ornaments | minor | [I from S yu-mistakes; M ours 8 in a chest patch] |
| PX-P17 | Silhouette line | 1 px, tinted near-black (L < 0.03) on 50-75% of the outer ring: a line everywhere, broken on the lit side by the material's own darkest tone (*changed in round FG1: was ">= 50%" with no upper bound; the idle measured 90%*) | major | [M refs 46–69% (pixel.md); FG1: 07 55%, 08 35%, 09 28%, 04 64% at L < 0.03, ours idle 90%, N1 73%, back 81%; finish-gap.md 2.2] |
| PX-P18 | Sel-out | lit side only, in the material's darkest tone; never where she meets the ground | minor | [S yu-mistakes, jansson, parmesan-aa] |
| PX-P19 | Bright pixels on the ring | the two brightest colours (G0, A5) ≤ 3% of ring pixels outside the rim-light lab | minor | [I from M ours 12%] |
| PX-P20 | Interior lines | lighter than the silhouette, in the material's deep tone; hair clump lines one step darker than the hair shadow | minor | [S jansson; QUALITY-RUBRIC 5] |
| PX-P21 | Step rhythm | line segments repeat a fixed ratio (1:1, 1:2, 1:3) or change steadily (1-1-2-3); no doubles on 1 px lines except deliberate weight | minor | [S saint11-2, jansson, rickyhan, mortmort] |
| PX-P22 | Anti-aliasing | interior curves only; only on steps ≥ 2 px; one halftone between the two colours; strip about half the step, max 2 px | minor | [S saint11-5, parmesan-aa; I exact numbers] |
| PX-P23 | Dithering | 0 dithered pixels on her. A dither alternates in two directions (a checker 2×2 with another beside or below it); a 1 px 45° line on a flat fill is a line, not a dither (*round R1: the detector had counted every 1:1 line*) | block | [S parmesan-dither, yu-mistakes, jansson] |

### 8.3 Materials (Rosace)

| ID | Material | Rule | Sev | Basis |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| PX-P24 | Skin | S1 small highlight, S2 lit, S3 shadow, S4 deep and cast; the shadow step must reach 1.6:1, so use S4 for cast shadows or darken S3 (palette owner) | major | [M S2/S3 1.42, S2/S4 2.50] |
| PX-P25 | White cloth | W1 is a highlight, not a base: flat panels sit in W2 so W1 can show form; folds are W3/W4 shapes with a sharp top edge; never neutral grey | major | [M ours 37% W1; QUALITY-RUBRIC 4] |
| PX-P26 | Stockings | the indigo ramp: I2 core, I1 sheen, I3 shadow, I4 deep; never the tabard's W ramp | block | [Dex; DESIGN 3 row 11] |
| PX-P27 | Gold trim | a trim meant to read as gold is 2 px (lit over shade), or a 1 px G2 line with the line tone beside it; never a 1 px run cycling G1/G2/G3/G0 | major | [S imonk, jansson; M ours] |
| PX-P28 | Boots, haft | glossy: one sharp I0 (or I1 on the haft) streak along the long axis | minor | [S jansson] |
| PX-P29 | Steel blade | T2–T4, with a 1 px A5 cutting edge | minor | [visible ref 08] |
| PX-P30 | Shadow budget per form | the terminator sits at a form's 35-45% quantile of the designed light and never past its 50% quantile; cast shadows reach at most 4 px at 144 (2 at 80); a form keeps a lit mass | major | [M shading S1: 62-71% of the body skin in shadow in N1/Q/back without it; visible refs 07/08] |
| PX-P31 | Dark thigh-highs | the stockings are the figure's darkest cloth: median HSV value 15-30%, median saturation <= 45% (near-black blue-violet, K ramp), a narrow cool sheen on the shin and knee, a darker welt band where the stocking grips the thigh | major | [M shading round 2: the round-2 relative-luminance test passed #3e3776 (V 46%) that reads as royal blue at 1x; critique param 14c; refs 04/08 stockings V ~20%] |
| PX-P32 | Value plan | weapon excluded, outline ring included: >= 25% of the figure at L < 0.10, >= 35% at L > 0.50, gold <= 10% | minor | [M shading round 2; refs 07/08/09 measured] (aspirational: no candidate passes yet) |
| PX-P33 | No 1 px shading clusters | no one-pixel tone cluster (8-connected) among the shading tones of skin and white, lines and override-layer pixels left out | major | [M shading round 2; S saint11-1] |
| PX-P34 | Rim inside the line | at world light no cyan on the outline ring; the rim sits on the first pixel inside it, on the side away from the key light and on convex forms only, in the local colour pushed toward the light (never `#ffffff`), <= 6% of the ring at 144; at 80 only the hair crown (DESIGN 9). *Changed in shading round 3: was <= 3% (26 px on the 144 idle read as "effectively no rim light", critique param 17)* | major | [M shading round 2 `rim_r2.json`, round 3 `rim_r3f.json`; critique round 2 param 17] |
| PX-P35 | No skin stripe | no one-tone skin cluster <= 2 px across its limb's axis and >= 6 px along it; a limb shades as a cylinder along its own axis (terminator at one u across the width, bending with the contour) with one short highlight lens | major | [critique round 1 param 14a; M shading round 2b `cyl_fit`] |
| PX-P36 | Value map, one accent | >= 12% of the opaque figure (weapon excluded) at V < 25%, and fewer than 3 palette colours at S > 60% and V > 30% holding >= 1% each: darks = hair shadow, stockings, boots; mids = skin, gold; lights = white cloth only; full saturation only for the eyes and the gem | major | [critique round 1 param 15; M shading round 2b] |
| PX-P37 | Separation on the combat backdrop | on the beige backdrop (`#dccab0`) the torso (bodice white and torso skin) separates by >= 2 value steps (a step = 1.3:1, PX-P07): its shadow tones and its silhouette line each at a median >= 1.69:1 against the backdrop, and <= 35% of its pixels within one step of it. *Added in shading round 3* | major | [critique round 2 param 15: the torso dissolved into the beige in N1 and Q; M shading round 3: o 0/8, pick 8/8] |
| PX-P38 | Coloured line on skin | the outline beside body skin is the skin's darkest shade (a warm red-umber), not the navy OL: <= 15% of the ring pixels whose only figure neighbours are body skin are OL (the face is FC-P18's); the navy OL stays on shadow-side outer tips and where she meets the ground. *Added in shading round 3* | major | [critique round 2 param 16: a sticker look; M shading round 3: o 0/8, the control 4/8, the pick 8/8] |
| PX-P39 | Chroma budget | keep the identity hues: whole-sprite mean HSV saturation 0.43-0.50 (within 0.03 of the integrated build), with saturated violet hair, true gold, lilac-shadowed white and dark indigo held as the hue anchors. The refs' lower chroma is reached by value, not by greying: big areas (stockings, boots, cloth shadows, hair underlayers) go low-key, and shadows turn cooler and more saturated, not grey. Accent pixels (S > 0.6 and V > 0.3) stay ≤ 5% where the gold allows (O-34). *Changed in round D9; old value: median S ≤ 0.35, chromatic pixels ≤ 70%, accent ≤ 5%, big areas near-neutral* | major | [M FG1: refs median S 0.15-0.42, ours 0.43-0.51; critic FJ1: F1 at median S 0.26 "destroyed her identity at 80", every judge kept the current colour identity; M D9: mean S 0.43-0.48 with the V < 0.35 share within 0.04 of current; finish-gap.md 2.3] |
| PX-P40 | Dark ramp | the stockings and boots (dark cloth) use 3-4 tones between L* 10 and 36 (each holding >= 10% of the material, outline excluded) with a mean HSV saturation <= 0.35 in both the darkest and lightest quarter. *Added in round FG1* | major | [M FG1: refs 3-4 tones over L* 10-36, S 0.13-0.31; ours 2 tones (L* 12, 19), S 0.40; finish-gap.md 2.4] |
| PX-P41 | Skin tones in use | the body and face skin use >= 4 tones covering 90% of the skin pixels (outline excluded; 1-D clusters of ±5 L*), with the shadow tones at a mean HSV S <= 0.35. *Added in round FG1* | major | [M FG1: refs 07/08/09/04 all 5 tones, shadow S 0.20-0.31; ours 3 tones, shadow S 0.43 (WebP control 4 tones: the codec adds at most one); finish-gap.md 2.4] |
| PX-P42 | Supersampled render to pixels | when a sprite comes from a render at 3-4x (route F1), alpha is binary (a pixel is figure where >= 45% of its block is), the silhouette is never blended with the background (0 in-between ring px), and in-between colours come only from interior blocks that hold two labels, then an adaptive palette per material group, the 2x2 checker break (PX-P23) and a despeckle run on the result. A plain box or bilinear downscale is never shipped. *Added in round FN1 (finish route F1)* | major | [M FN1: P0-P4 at 144 and 80 read as pixel art at 1x with alpha only 0/255 and ring AA 0; the lines and the face stamp, drawn after the quantise, made new two-way checkers (1 dithered area on N1 in every preset) until the checker pass ran again last; P0 back keeps 1; `finish_f1/f1_post.py`; S saint11-5, parmesan-aa (no silhouette AA)] |
| PX-P43 | Painterly ramps around the identity | a painterly material ramp is 5-11 stops interpolated in OKLab, anchored on the build's own key colours: shadows step cooler and more saturated (violet/indigo), highlights warmer; lit white is near-white from the white's median ramp input (0.40 on the v2 build), never a lavender mid; the skin terminator is one narrow saturated red-orange step (≤ 0.1 of the ramp input wide) with cool rose shadows under it; gold runs orange-brown to near-white-yellow; dark cloth is violet-black with a sheen only in the top 10% of the input. *Added in round D9* | major | [critic FJ1 fixes; M D9: the first white ramp put 50% of the lit cloth at #b6b0d8, a wide terminator turned the back view's skin brick-red; visible x3/x8 trial sheets] |
| PX-P44 | A line on every light shape | under a selective outline a light material (white, veil, beige, gold, skin) keeps a dark ring all round: its lit side takes the material's deepest ramp stop (skin: a warm umber mixed with it), never a tone near the ground's value; only dark materials take a lighter sel-out on the lit side. *Added in round D9* | major | [critic FJ1: "F1 lost the outline on the white sleeves and bust against the grey background"; refs 07/09 visible; M D9 ring dark share 0.57-0.74] |
| PX-P45 | Low-key numbers, measured every round | the painterly chain's figure measures an HSV p90 value ≤ 0.90 and a mean saturation ≤ 0.42 at 144 and at 80. For reference, refs 08 and 04 measure p90 0.85 and 0.91, refs 07 and 09 0.66 and 0.69, and the refs' saturation runs 0.19-0.36. Get there by capping each ramp's top stop under paper white, a per-material gamma and an OKLab chroma scale, so the hues stay (PX-P39); never grey the whole sprite. *Added in round D9-R2* | major | [critic D9-R1 materials 15: "value p90 0.90 against 0.66-0.69, saturation 0.42 against 0.19-0.36"; M D9-R2: p90 0.97 → 0.88-0.92, S 0.45 → 0.40-0.45, 192-201 colours at 144; the idle's 0.92 and N1's and the back's 0.44-0.45 still fail] |
| PX-P46 | The rim lives on the outline pixel | in the painterly chain the rim light is drawn **on** the outline pixel of curved forms (skin, hair, white, veil, dark cloth) on the side facing the rim direction, as the material's 0.72 tone mixed about 55% toward the rim colour and capped under 230, with 1 px gaps bridged and runs under 3 px dropped. It is never a second light line one pixel inside the outline. *Added in round D9-R3* (DESIGN revision 2 already did this for the white cloth) | major | [critic D9-R2 rim 17: "a strong dashed cyan line inset 1 px from the outline on both legs and tabard: a double outline that flattens; merge it into the outline, make it continuous"; M D9-R3: 150-180 rim px on the outline at 144, 0 inset] |
| PX-P47 | Texture as clusters, not grit | painterly texture comes from noise blurred to clusters before the ramp (brush about 1.4 px, strands 0.4 x 1.6 px at the sprite scale, spread restored), then a despeckle pass: a fill pixel with no same-colour 4-neighbour in its own material takes its material's most common 8-neighbour colour (lines, rim, face and glass kept). The haft takes no texture at all. *Added in round D9-R3* | major | [critic D9-R2 materials 14 / pixel craft 16: "speckle noise reads as dither grit ... replace per-pixel noise with 2-3 px directional clusters; min cluster 2 px"; M D9-R3 idle 144: flat neighbour steps 0.27 → 0.41, hard steps 0.34 → 0.30 (ref 08 0.61 / 0.13 by the critic's measure)] |
| PX-P48 | Attack stills carry their effect stage | an attack still shows the effect the moveset gives that frame (MOVESET 0.6: full, leaded, shards), drawn in code on the background only and left out of the silhouette and colour counts. For a thrust or scoop, the smear is the blade's swept fan (width about the blade's length, tapering) about the rear fist, not a thin crescent about the front fist: a thin crescent hanging off the tip reads as a second, scythe-like blade. *Added in round D9-R3* | major | [critics D9-R2 stylisation 11 and first impression: "no stretch, no smear ... the refs sell the hit with big FX"; trials D9-R3 on N1: a 14-16 px crescent about the front or rear fist read as a scythe blade; a 28 px fan about the rear fist reads as a swept smear] |

### 8.4 Pixel negative rules

| ID | Never / avoid | Why | Sev | Basis |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| PX-N01 | **Never** pillow-shade: no shadow ring following the outline all round a shape | kills the light direction; puffy and blurry | block | [S yu-mistakes, saint11-4, parmesan-aa] |
| PX-N02 | **Never** let two tones band (a run of one colour hugging an equal run of another along a contour) | reinforces the grid; reads as 3D toon steps | major | [S parmesan-aa, azzi-pixel-logic secondary; M] |
| PX-N03 | **Never** use pure `#000000` on her | enemy black (DESIGN 4) | block | [DESIGN 4] |
| PX-N04 | **Never** AA the outer silhouette, a 1:1 line or a straight line | unknown background; blur | major | [S saint11-5, parmesan-aa] |
| PX-N05 | **Never** put two light materials next to each other at the same value without a line or shadow tone between | they merge at 1x: the white-on-white | block | [S jansson, steam-grey; M] |
| PX-N06 | **Avoid** muddy darks: a dark tone within 1.3:1 of the outline used as a mass | it merges into the line | minor | [S saint11-6, jansson; M I4/OL] |
| PX-N07 | **Avoid** 5 or more tones within L ± 0.25 in any 6×6 window | pixels blur together | minor | [S yu-mistakes] |
| PX-N08 | **Avoid** 1 px parts that need shading (a 1 px strap with a ramp) | can't hold light and shadow | minor | [S yu-mistakes] |
| PX-N09 | **Avoid** rendered detail the eye can't use at 1x (tiny folds, extra trim) | every detail is copied into every frame | minor | [S saint11-4, jansson] |
| PX-N10 | **Avoid** parallel light bands on planes that face different ways (the same W1 stripe down the tabard and both legs) | reads as one flat surface | minor | [S onimille] |
| PX-N11 | **Never** keep the render's shadow boundary as-is | surface noise becomes blotches; the terminator must be a designed shape | major | [S motomura-ggxrd] |
| PX-N12 | **Never** shade a hanging flat panel (tabard, stole) as a cylinder: no highlight stripe along its length, one shadow side or a fold shape | parallel strips down a panel are the figure's worst banding and read as a tube | major | [M shading S1 hug map; S onimille] |
| PX-N13 | **Never** double the outline: a deep tone just inside a dark silhouette line in runs of >= 3 px | reads as a 2 px outline, heavy and flat | major | [M shading round 2] |

---

## 9. Self-check before a drawing is done

Run in this order, at 6x and again at 1x, beside 07/08/09 at the same scale. A "no" goes back to
the step that owns it (WF-P06). The IDs point to the rule; `checklist.json` says whether a
script can answer it.

**A. Gesture (on the wireframe, before any pixels)**

1. One C or S from crown to weight heel, sagitta in range? (FG-P07)
2. Shoulders and hips tilt opposite ways, each ≥ 2 px? (FG-P08, FG-P09, FG-N01)
3. Plumb line from the pit of the neck on the weight foot? (FG-P11)
4. One leg vertical, the other bent? (FG-P10, FG-N02)
5. Head tilted or turned? (FG-P12)
6. Arms doing different jobs? (FG-P16, FG-N03)

**B. Masses and proportion**

7. Head count, crotch, knees, elbows on their marks? (FG-P01 to FG-P04)
8. Rib cage narrower than the pelvis; waist about 15 px? (FG-P05)
9. In a twist, is the centre line an S? (FG-P17)

**C. Silhouette (fill her solid; 1x and 3x)**

10. Action and facing read with no interior detail? (critic, QUALITY-RUBRIC 1; [S valve-tf2,
    secondary])
11. Arm–torso gaps: ≥ 1 in an idle, ≥ 2 in a hero or strike pose? (FG-P13, FG-N04)
12. Width ≥ 0.40 H? Feet apart in range? (FG-P14, FG-P15)
13. Weapon clear by 4–8 px or overlapping by ≥ 3 px; no 1–2 px slivers? (GR-P10, FG-N06)
14. No flagpole, no haft through the centre band, no coat hanger? (GR-N02, GR-N03, GR-N04)
15. Flip it. Anything lopsided you'd stopped seeing? (WF-P08)

**D. Hands and weapon**

16. Each gripping hand wraps the haft with a thumb wedge and a curved knuckle row? (HD-P01,
    HD-P02, GR-P06, HD-N01)
17. Both fists on one haft line, the haft collinear through them? Wrist step on the fist, thumb
    inside it? (GR-P05, HD-P07)
18. Grip spacing right for the move; rear hand at the hip? (GR-P01 to GR-P04)
19. Every wrist inside the limits? (HD-P06, HD-N05)
20. Does the weapon's weight show in the shoulders or lean? (GR-P08)
21. Rest angle 15–35°, leaning away from her? (GR-P09)

**E. Cloth and outfit**

22. Every fold starts at a tension point; the tabard follows the tilted pelvis? (CL-P01, CL-P02,
    CL-N01)
23. Every white-on-white overlap separated; a dark value between the legs? (CL-P03, CL-P04)
24. The collar cross reads and nothing covers it? (CL-P05)
25. Neck or shoulder visible on at least one side? (CL-N02)
25a. Sleeves at DESIGN size with lining, hem and folds from the elbow; no bell hanging across the
    torso? (CL-P06, CL-N03)

**F. Face (at 10x and 1x)**

26. Grid rows and face ratio on target? (FC-P01, FC-P02)
27. Both irises aimed at a named target; highlights on the same side? (FC-P11, FC-P12, FC-N01,
    FC-N21)
28. Eyes open on the default face; half-lid only on Serene? (FC-P13, FC-N22)
29. Lash 2 rows with a flick; lower lash a dot? (FC-P09)
30. Far eye narrower; far brow shorter? (FC-P04, FC-P15)
31. Brows present; mouth with a bent corner; nothing else in S4? (FC-P15, FC-P07, FC-N16)
32. Chin point 2–4 px off-centre; no straight cheek run over 3 rows? (FC-P08, FC-N13)
33. No hair between the eyes below the lash; a skin gap between bang clumps above each eye?
    (FC-N17, HR-P04)
34. At least one asymmetry; each expression changes two zones? (FC-P20, FC-N19)
34a. Dark iris top under the lash, one white column; if the eye line moved, did the bang tips move
    with it? Profile: nose tip, lip and eye set-back on their rows? (FC-P22, FC-P21, FC-P23)
34b. Smug or tired? Iris top A2 with an A4 crescent; the lash not falling at the outer end; the brows not
    raised at the inner end; the smirk visible in S4; no vertical marks under the eyes; the eye line rolled
    1 px; the fringe unequal, with no straight parallel hair lines (FC-P22, FC-N25, FC-N26, FC-P07, FC-N24,
    FC-P25, HR-P11, HR-N04)
34c. The near eye the bigger and brighter at 1x; both catchlights on the top iris row at one offset from the pupils;
    brows in clean windows under a solid fringe shadow, no speckle; the jaw runs shortening into the chin; the crown
    dashes on different rows; a lit break in the silhouette, no dark twig (FC-P26, FC-P12, FC-P27, FC-N27, FC-N28,
    HR-P08, HR-P13, HR-N05)
35. Next to 07/08/09 at 3x, would a stranger call hers the same kind of face, not a mask? Run the whole-face pick
    beside ref 08 at x3 and 1x; no checker pass overrides it (WF-P11, critic)

**G. Rendering**

36. Greyscale and squint: 3 value groups; legs separate from the tabard; skin from white?
    (PX-P09, PX-N05)
37. One light; one terminator per form; cast shadows designed? (PX-P01 to PX-P03, PX-N11)
38. Pillow and band hunt: `pixel_metrics.py` banding ≤ 3.5 per 1,000 px? (PX-N01, PX-P14)
39. First shadow steps ≥ 1.6:1; W1 a highlight, not a base? (PX-P06, PX-P25)
40. Orphans, thin shadow shapes, dense windows? (PX-P12, PX-P13, PX-P16)
41. Lines: 1 px, steady rhythm, no doubles, dark ring ≥ 50%, no bright ring pixels? (PX-P17 to
    PX-P21)
42. AA interior only; no dithering? (PX-P22, PX-P23)

**H. Attitude and appeal**

43. Cover the face: does the body alone say serene, certain, ceremonial? (critic)
44. Any twin left? (FG-N03, FC-N18)
45. Would a critic call it stiff? If there's doubt, push one step further and A/B. (FG-N09)
46. Against the ref at the same size, name one thing theirs does better and fix it first.
    (WF-P07)
47. Did a thumbnail move the head or the neck? Score it on CL-P05 and FG-P12 before calling it the hero (WF-P03).

---

## 10. Learning log

Every round adds rows here: what was tried, what failed, why (with the evidence), and the rule
it produced or changed. A rule that changes keeps its ID; the row says what the old value was.
A new rule gets the next free ID in its area and goes into `checklist.json` in the same edit.

| Round | Date | What was tried | What failed | Why (evidence) | Rule produced or changed |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| C1 | 2026-09-29 | Line of action drawn crown → weight hip joint → weight heel | FG-P07: sagitta 1.1 px | With a vertical weight leg and the pit of the neck over the foot, the hip *joint* sits on the chord. The curve has to pass the outer hip (the thrust), and the weight leg leans in up to FG-P10's 5° [M, rules_check] | No rule change. Construction: the line of action runs through the weight-side pelvis edge; the pelvis shifts toward the weight side and the heel sits under the pit of the neck |
| C1 | 2026-09-29 | Free hand on the collar cross, both arm segments at full length | FG-N04: far arm had no gap; the elbow hung 2 px from the ribs | The hand is 6–11 px from the shoulder, so a full-length upper arm can only fold straight down. In 3/4 the far upper arm points away from the camera and looks shorter [M; S han-wide on foreshortening] | No rule change. Construction: per-segment foreshortening (far upper arm 0.62, forearm 0.95) lifts the elbow out; the window is now 150+ px² |
| C1 | 2026-09-29 | Six gesture thumbnails, scored on 9 silhouette rules; kept t4 (glaive 25°, lower grip) | Built in full, t4's fist sat 3.2 px off the haft: the grip was out of reach | The thumbnail score didn't include the grip [M] | WF-P03 in practice: the thumbnail score now includes GR-P05 and GR-P09 (11 rules). Re-run kept t5 (hand on hip) and t2 (wide stance). The hero took t2's stance and kept the collar hand (DESIGN 1); t5 is the A/B alternative. The control thumbnail (level shoulders and hips, locked knees) scored last, 7/11, so the score does catch the mannequin |

---

## 11. Open questions

- **O-1 Head size.** The face grid (FC-P01) wants hair top to chin 29–30 px, which means moving
  the chin down 2–3 px (the refs' faces below the lash are 13–14.5 px, ours 11). With a 2–3 px
  hair volume that makes skull top to chin 26–28 px, against FG-P01's 24 px. The refs' 24–25 px
  heads in REF-BREAKDOWN may have been measured to a different skull top. Re-measure the refs'
  skull line on the 07/08 portrait sheets before locking; until then the face grid wins for the
  face, and the head count is checked in the 5.5–6.5 band. [unknown]
- **O-2 Eye-line tilt.** The refs' 1 px tilt is at the edge of WebP noise. Confirm on the
  larger portrait sheets before making it a lock. [unknown]
- **O-3 Highlight touching the white.** PIPELINE 3.2 (from critics) says the highlight never
  touches the white; Konart teaches that at least one highlight sits where the iris meets the
  white, so the shine reads as on the cornea. A/B both on the first constructed face and record
  the winner in the learning log. [conflict]
- **O-4 Near-eye width.** face.md measured 5–7 px on the refs and proposed 6; PIPELINE 3.2 says
  5. FC-P04 allows 5–6; pick by A/B. [conflict] **Answered for q34 in round R1: 6** (the 7 px
  variant failed FC-P04/P10 with no visible gain; the front view uses 5 with a 5 px gap).
- **O-5 Unread source.** st0ven's "Fighter Sprite Faces (female)" is exactly our genre but
  couldn't be fetched; read it by hand and fold in anything new.
- **O-7 Banding on thin line parts.** PX-P14 counts hugging runs on the haft. A 1–3 px diagonal
  band made of two or more tones hugs itself at every column step, whatever the artist does (log
  round C1). Either PX-P14 excludes parts at most 3 px thick, or the haft is drawn in one tone plus
  broken glints. Re-measure the refs' weapons (07's wrench handle, 09's katana) with the same
  metric before deciding. [unknown] **Answered in round R1:** PX-P14 and PX-N02 leave out line
  parts at most 3 px thick; PX-P21 judges them. The refs' weapons are broad blades, not 1 px-core
  hafts, so their whole-figure numbers don't transfer [visible].
- **O-10 The free hand at rest.** DESIGN 1 puts it at the collar cross. With the bell sleeves at
  DESIGN size (CL-P06) that pose fails FG-N04 and covers the chest window (CL-N03, log R1); the hand
  on the far hip passes everything and reads with more attitude. Keep the hip (and edit DESIGN 1),
  or keep the collar and pin the far bell back? [Dex]
- **O-11 Interior lines in PX-P13.** PX-P13 counts every S3/W3/I3/G3 component that is never
  2 px thick, so 1 px interior lines drawn in a shadow tone (hair clump lines, pipe folds, lacing)
  count as thin shadow shapes. PX-P20 treats them as lines. Should PX-P13 skip interior lines
  (1 px, not touching the outline) and keep counting rims along the outline? Measure the refs' clump
  lines first. [unknown]
- **O-12 Boot height.** DESIGN 3 row 12 gives the boots 9 px at 96 (about 13.5 at 144, an ankle
  boot), but calls them mid-calf; both constructs draw 20–21 px. [conflict, design]
- **O-13 The critic's A1 iris top.** The round-1 critique asked for an A1 iris top at 2:1 or more against
  OL; A1 measures 1.58:1 [M]. **Answered in round R2:** A2 (3.24:1); FC-P22 changed.
- **O-14 Banding on the painted hair.** PX-P14 rose from 5.3 to 8.8 per 1,000 px in round R2; most hugging
  pairs are the head's hair lines and the chest-window frame. Either the hair lines need more bends and fewer
  parallel runs, or PX-P14 should weigh the head separately from the body. Measure the refs' heads with the same
  metric first. [unknown]
- **O-15 The 3D render as a shading reference.** On the before/after sheet the round-4-fix still carries richer
  form shading on the torso and legs than the R2 paint passes. WF-P02 lets the render supply form; should the
  paint passes take their terminator shapes from the render's shadow boundary (redrawn as designed shapes, PX-N11)
  instead of from row-by-row fractions? [Dex]
- **O-16 The profile's crown flick.** HR-P13's lit break at the front of the profile crown reads a little like an ear at x7
  (round R3 by eye). Keep it, move it back along the crown, or give the profile its break at the back? Judge in motion. [I]
- **O-17 The brow window is one row deep.** Every WF-P04 eye-line or brow variant fails FC-P27 (round R3): at 144 the fringe,
  its shadow band, the brow and the lash share about 5 rows. Either the variants move the fringe with the brow (as FC-P21 does
  for the eye line), or the grid gives the forehead one more row (O-1). [unknown]
- **O-18 The figure's finish gap.** Round R3 moved the face and the stance; the figure's torso, bells and legs are still pale
  and flat-shaded next to 07/08/09 (PX-P13, PX-P14, PX-N02, the palette items of O-6). The refs carry large dark value masses
  and hand-placed form shading; the next round should run the WF-P11 pick on whole figures with render variants, not only
  on gestures. [I]
- **O-8 Boot shadow tone.** DESIGN 3 row 12 gives the boot an I4 shadow side. PX-N06 caps any
  tone within 1.3:1 of OL at a 20 px patch (I4/OL is 1.25:1); the constructed boot's shadow side
  measured 22–27 px. Either the boot shadow becomes I3 over I4 (a design edit), or PX-N06 exempts
  the boots. [conflict]
- **O-9 Far sidelock length.** CL-N02 wants neck or shoulder visible on one side; DESIGN 6 has both
  sidelocks reaching the collarbone. The construct cuts the far one under the jaw (log round C1).
  Dex's call. [Dex]
- **O-6 Palette owner actions.** PX-P06 and PX-P24 need the first skin and white shadow steps
  to reach 1.6:1, and PX-P26 needs `palette.json`'s `stocking` material moved to the indigo
  ramp. Those are palette edits, not rule edits.

- **O-19 Trim lines in PX-P14** (outfit round 1). A 1 px gold trim held at one tone (CL-P07) beside a flat
  fill is a hugging run pair, so the banding count rises as the trim gets cleaner (7.2 → 8.4 per 1,000 px on
  the idle). Should PX-P14 leave out 1 px trim lines as O-7 left out the haft?
- **O-20 The face grid on the v2 head** (face round F1). FC-P01/P02/N09 were measured on refs 07/08/09 at a 29.5 px head (hair top to chin); the v2 head is 26-27 px with the eye bottom 6-7 rows over the chin, so every v2 face fails them by 2-4 rows while it reads right at x3 and 1x. Scale the grid rows by head height (hair top to chin / 29.5), move the drawn eyes up 1-2 px and cut more fringe (tried: reads older and bald at the brows), or change the head? [Dex]
- **O-21 An 80 px face rule set** (face round F1). The FC rules are written at 144; at the 80 px world height a face is 2-row eyes, no brows and a 2-3 px mouth, so FC-P13/P15/N23 fail by construction. Write 80 px targets (ref 05's 14 px heads: a dark 2 px eye, one catchlight, no brows on the fringe) or judge 80 by the WF-P11 pick only? [I]
- **O-22 The glaive's size (round G1).** The WF-P11 pick went to the l3 glaive in 6 of 6 sheets: the rose disc
  10.5 du across (DESIGN 7: 9) and the blade 31 du long (28), with the haft 2.4 du thick. Adopt the bigger disc and blade
  into DESIGN 7, or keep DESIGN's numbers and take l2 (same haft, 9 du disc)? [Dex]
- **O-23 The idle's legs against the staff (round G1).** At the reachable lean (16.8°) the haft is 9° off both thighs
  (GR-N02, block): the free thigh steps toward the staff at about 26°. Either the free leg steps less or the other way
  (pose owner), or the body leans onto the staff so the grip reaches past 18° (a 20° lean needs the shoulder about 5 cm
  nearer). The refs' gripping hands are dark gloves (07, 09): dark gauntlet cuffs or gloves would also make the fists
  read at 1x; that is a costume call. [Dex]

- **O-24 Outfit pose patches** (outfit round 2). `art/rosace/overrides/global/outfit/pose_patches.json` holds drape keys the
  outfit needs (Q's tabard between the thighs, the back view's tabard behind the far leg) and one it only proposes (the back
  view's hair tail swept aside, which shows the waist and seat but still hides the yoke cross). Integrate folds them into the
  pose files; the hair tail is the hair lane's and the pose owner's call. Round O3 adds `hair4` (the tail and back mass swept over the near shoulder in N2: the spine and upper back show) and finds the veil, not the hair, now hides the back yoke cross; the veil chain would need a key too. The tabard's S-bend with lag needs a per-link wind in `posing.drape`. [I]

- **O-25 Eye rules against the F2 eye** (face round F2). The critique asked for a 1 px lash with a 2 px flick rising from the outer end, a far eye narrower but not smaller, and the smirk-side lid lifted; the pick does all three, and the checker fails it on FC-P09/FC-N06 (the diagonal flick counts as 3 lash rows), FC-P26 (near 4 rows / 14 px against far 4 / 13: near dominance wants 1 more row) and FC-P24 (the lift is measured on the near eye). Change the measurements to the critique, or the eye to the rules? [I]

- **O-26 Brows under a covering fringe** (face round F2). FC-P15 and FC-N23 (block) require brows "always present, drawn over the bangs"; the F1 critique rejected both carved windows and brows over the fringe, and on every v2 still the fringe covers the brow line, so F2 draws none and lets the lids carry the mood. Scope FC-P15/N23 to faces whose fringe shows the brow line, or ask the hair lane for real gaps between the bang clumps over each eye? [Dex]

- **O-27 Stance width: DESIGN's 1.3–1.6 shoulder-widths against the glamour refs' narrow stance**
  (figure-pose round FP0, `art-rules/pose.md` 5). Ref 04's standing figures measure about
  0.2–0.8 sw heel to heel, with the knees together and a foot often crossing. The model guides
  cross the ankle and rest the foot on the toe. The combat refs 07 and 08 stand 1.7–1.8 sw
  [M, by eye]. PS-P06 follows DESIGN 3.5 and adds the knee rule (the knee gap at most 0.6 × the
  heel gap). A narrow "catwalk" variant (0.4–0.7 sw, the free foot crossing in front on its toe)
  goes into the blind A/B. If the critics pick it, the stance number is Dex's call. [Dex]
- **O-28 The critique's mouth and brows against R3's rules** (face round F2b). The round-2 critique asks for a 2-3 px mouth with one raised corner and 1 px brows over the fringe; FC-P07/FC-N11 (round R3) require a 4 px Confident mouth, and FC-P27 (round R3) wants skin under every brow px (a window, which the F1 critique rejected). The F2b pick draws a 3 px smirk and OL brows over the fringe, so all three fail by construction. Change FC-P07 to 3-4 px and scope FC-P27 to faces whose fringe shows the brow line, or keep R3's rules? [I]

- **O-29 GR-P03 on the Q stamp** (glaive-hands round G2). GR-P03 puts both hands within 8–14 px of the haft's middle for twirls and Q; in the crouched Q plant the haft's middle projects at her head height, so the rule as written asks for a fist on the face (round 1's one high fist, which the critique rejected). The G2 pick holds the haft with both fists under the chin, 33 and 13 px below the middle. Scope GR-P03 to twirls (N3) and give Q its own spacing (both fists between the chin and the navel, 12–24 px apart), or re-pose Q so the middle drops (a shorter plant)? [I]

- **O-30 The veil in the idle** (hair round H2). HR-P17 asks for 12+ veil px past the hair in the idle 3/4 view; critique H1-7 asks for one continuous veil shape and no white chips in the hair. With the r2 hair (1-2 px more volume) a veil big enough to show in the idle covers the back hair in N2 (k 1.34), and the r2f veil shows 6 px in the idle, which the pass removes as chips. Drop the idle clause (the veil is a back-view read), draw a 2 px veil edge behind the head as a pixel rule, or shrink the back hair? [I]

- **O-31 PX-P18 (sel-out on the lit side only) against the round-2 critique's line fix** (shading round 3). Critique param 16 asks for the outline in the darkest shade of the fill all round (skin red-umber, white slate-lavender, gold umber) with the navy OL only on shadow-side corners; PX-P18 as written wants the sel-out on the lit side only, so the round-3 pick fails it on 8/8 stills by construction (o passed 8/8 and lost param 16). Rewrite PX-P18 as "sel-out in the fill's darkest shade on the whole contour; OL on shadow-side tips and ground contact" (PX-P38 measures it), or keep it? [I]

---
- **O-32 Concept A calibration conflicts** (figure-pose explore A). Four numbers the kept A pose can't meet together, for Dex and the critics: (1) PS-P06's 35 px heel floor at 144 is out of reach with a locked weight leg, a ≥ 10° free knee and ≤ 8 px heel lift on this body (max about 32-33 px, 1.2 sw); proposal: a 30 px floor (17 at 80), or measure at the pointed toe. (2) PS-P01's 80 px drops (2-3 px at the hip) assume a front-on hip; at pelvis yaw 45-50 the ASIS line is about 6 px, so scale the drop by the on-screen width. (3) GR-P09 / PS-P15's 15-35° leaning away can't hold with a head-height grip on the free-leg side; the A-frame (14° leaning in, butt beyond the free foot) is what frames her there. (4) PS-P17 as enclosed holes: an open λ never has one, so measure bays.

- **O-33 Concept B's leg-scale cheat** (figure-pose explore B). The kept `idle_appeal_B` meets PS-P06 (heels 36.5 px at 144) only with the free leg scaled 1.10 (PS-P20's per-pose cheat); without it the same pose tops out near 19-27 px (O-32 (1)). The far leg is 10 % longer and thicker in this one still. Is that an acceptable cheat for the idle and the 80 px world idle, or should the stance floor move instead (O-32)? The blind variant sheets in `review/rosace/art/figure-pose/explore/B/blind/` show B1 (no cheat) beside B5 (the cheat).

- **O-34 Gold share and the colour budget** (round FG1). Gold covers 19.6% of the idle sprite (glaive included), against PX-P32's <= 10%, and gives 7.9 of its 11.8% accent pixels (PX-P39). PX-P40 and PX-P41 need about 4-6 more colours (skin and neutral dark steps). Take them from the saturated indigo/azure steps inside PX-P15's 32, or raise PX-P15 to 40? The refs don't argue for a larger cap once the codec is allowed for (finish-gap.md 2.1), so the first is the default [I]

- **O-35 PX-P15 and PX-P39 under the painterly finish** (round FN1, finish route F1). The F1 pick measures 43-45 colours at 144 (38-40 at 80) and the 32-colour budget still lands at 36-38 once the outline, skin line and face stamp colours are added; its N1 chromatic share is 0.78 because skin and gold alone are about 70% of that sprite. Does PX-P15 count line and face-stamp colours inside the 32 (then F1 needs a shared line ramp), or cap the fill colours only? And does PX-P39's chromatic share exclude skin (the refs' skin is chromatic too), or should the N1 gold shrink (O-34)? [I]
